# 引擎代码审查补充发现（2026-06）

> 规范权威：跨域通用规则已统一收敛至 [Zircon 开发规范总纲](zircon_runtime/frameworks/development-conventions.md)；本文保留代码审查发现、专题论证与执行上下文，不再作为并列规则源。

## 产出记录迁移说明

> 请将产出记录放置在子计划中，此处仅展示当前现状的概述

具体补记、验证与修复记录已迁入 [`_archive/zircon_runtime/runtime/15/2026-07-09-engine-code-review-findings-output-records.md`](_archive/zircon_runtime/runtime/15/2026-07-09-engine-code-review-findings-output-records.md)。本文件仅保留审查发现、优先级与当前现状概述。

当前 G7 前置元数据收敛（2026-08-09）：本文件 frontmatter 已删除 185 条不存在的历史 owner（`related_code` 115、`implementation_files` 69、已删除 `frontmatter_status.rs` guard 1），不恢复旧路径、兼容入口或状态镜像；正文及并发 Text04 状态更新保持原样。`check_conventions --only docs` 对本文件的悬空路径从 185 项降为 0；同批五份 G7 目标文档均为 0，但共享工作树仍为 RED，且全局计数随外来 owner 并发输入变化而不在本 exact scope 冻结，因此本文继续保持 `in_progress`，不把局部清理表述为全库验收。

当前 plan-status 审查同步（2026-07-10）：已修复“父计划/总索引重复持有历史具体产出”的结构漂移，并恢复 index 的允许聚合表面。具体证据由编号归档拥有，父文档只保留当前概述；archive reader 与 parent-routing guard 均为 folder-backed child owner。Python audit `risks = []`，standalone Rust plan-status 48/48；未新增大文件职责、兼容层或生产行为旁路。

当前审查行概述：编号审查归档负责维护各发现的证据状态与验证结果；本总览不复制状态锚、数量或门禁结果。各项是否完成以及尚待执行的工程门统一以编号 Runtime 15 产出记录为准。

当前 Frameworks 审查同步（2026-07-31）：结构/P1 发现的物理收口不再引用 2026-07-02 的声明顺序注释或 graphics→scene/UI 旧计数。[`Frameworks 01 M0`](zircon_runtime/frameworks/01/2026-07-13-m0-current-structure-and-dependency-baseline.md) 已用 9,188 个 Rust 输入 pre/post 同指纹 `348fe59c72c798e5e64babb5489910f30dbd00a58441ec11c1e176826519339e` 固化 2,391 refs / 76 edges 时点快照。M1 的 foundation prefix 是 `zr_math/zr_resource → zr_contracts → zr_kernel → zr_diagnostics`；完整物理 DAG 继续按 `foundation → platform/input → asset/scene → zr_operation → rhi/...`，随后 `zr_text → graphics text backend/graphics → ui/optional`。`zr_operation` 是 scene 之后、可选 navigation 之前的内部 layer-3 owner，因为当前 operation 真实消费 `CoreHandle` 与 `scene::World`；`zr_text` 只拥有 backend-neutral shaping/layout/font/atlas policy，GPU upload/draw 归 graphics/RHI backend，禁止 text 直连完整 wgpu/naga/glyphon 或形成循环。审查必须阻止 `asset→text=2`、`scene→animation=2`、`rhi→rhi_wgpu=1` 三条时点反向边进入新 crate，并先把 source-cubemap projection 的两处 concrete Runtime `TaskPool` 测试移交 kernel integration owner。公开 facade 只做最终 curated surface，不能成为旧内部 owner、alias、shim 或兼容双轨。M4 同时执行 stable-first：当前 `notify 9.0.0-rc.3`、`winit 0.31.0-beta.2`、`zip 9.0.0-pre2` 只能以精确 package/version、owner、理由、到期日与 ticket 的限期例外存在；过期例外保持 RED，禁止 wildcard、整类禁用或 advisory 静默。该同步状态为 `static-baseline-implemented / timings-pending / physical-migration-not-started`；共享源码继续演进时，物理迁移前必须重新原子采集，不能把本时点快照冒充永久 current source。

Frameworks 审查增量（2026-08-03）：`rhi→rhi_wgpu=1` 已通过物理 `zr_rhi <- zr_rhi_wgpu` 单向依赖前向修复，不以 alias、shim、dev-dependency 或 Runtime private module 掩盖。`zr_rhi` manifest 无 WGPU，`zr_rhi_wgpu` 独占 concrete presenter/timer/readback 与 deterministic backend tests，Runtime facade 显式投影 neutral symbols 并保留唯一 default presenter factory，Editor 未获得 concrete backend API。旧 `src/rhi`/`src/rhi_wgpu` owner 和 `crate::rhi_wgpu` import 均已删除；静态 server boundary 12/12 与 dependency audit GREEN。审查结论仍为 `implementation-complete / managed-validation-pending`，在 `cargo tree -p zr_rhi`、两 crate focused tests、Runtime15 path guards、Runtime/Editor lib gates和独立二次审查完成前不得把 failure 改为 fixed 或把 Frameworks01 M2 标为 accepted。历史 `rhi_wgpu/command_validation.rs`、`rhi_wgpu/device.rs`、`rhi_wgpu/ui_surface.rs` 与 `rhi/tests/...` 字符串只保留为 former-owner 产出记录，不构成当前路径或兼容承诺。

当前文本计划同步(2026-08-24):按本文“受 guard 计划由 owner 吸收”的规则，runtime text 的 rich/vertical prewarm 状态由 Text09 所有；Text04 的 native bitmap 路径已硬切为 canonical shaped glyph → `GlyphRasterKey` → bounded Swash worker → `GlyphAtlasSet`。`scene_renderer/ui/text/native_glyph_run.rs` 负责一次 handle 解析、glyph projection 和 font-id 诊断复用；renderer 不接收字符串、glyphon `Buffer`/`TextArea` 或 `CacheKey`。`ScreenSpaceUiTextPrepareReport.raster_upload` 聚合层继续由 folder-backed prepare-report test 覆盖，source-cache 维持 O(1) LRU 和至多三次纵向近似 probe。详细验证仍归档在 `docs/plans/zircon_runtime/text/**` 与 `docs/tests/runtime/text`。受管 Cargo、真实 WGPU framebuffer、GPU/功耗测量仍未完成，不提前表述为验收通过。

当前文本基础设施同步（2026-07-17）：共享 FontDatabase 重复发布造成的全局 generation 抖动已在最低 owner 修复。`text/font/database/equivalence.rs` 只比较会改变渲染的语义输入，`text/font/shared.rs` 在同一写锁内完成等价判定、替换与 generation 迁移；等价的 screen-space renderer 初始化不再清空 shaping/SDF resident cache。系统字体 `Discover` 同时在 FontDatabase owner 内幂等化，renderer clone 不再重复目录扫描或向 `fontdb` backend 追加重复 face；`TextRenderState` 也已按 text-owned system locale 直接从共享 backend DB 构造 cosmic FontSystem，删除临时 `FontSystem::new()` 的第二次系统扫描。三项 system-policy regressions 已按 owner 拆入 `text/font/database/tests/system_policy.rs`，不继续堆叠近预算测试根。generation-sensitive SDF tests 已按职责拆入 `text/sdf/font_bake/tests/cache_generation.rs`，生产路径无新增锁、无 facade/shim、无旧双轨，`font/database.rs` 与 `font_bake.rs` 仍低于 1000 行硬限。同一 MVP 稳定性切片还删除 `text/parallel/raster_pool.rs::request(...)` 的 production `expect`：sender 缺失时在 work id 入队前返回 `CoreError::ChannelSend`，不污染 in-flight 诊断；primary fallback resolver 与 SDF ensure/map 异常态也改为在各自最低 owner fail-closed，不再生产 panic。raster worker 同时硬切固定 `page_generation=0` 伪 target：CPU bitmap 只按 face epoch 失效，真实 page generation 继续由 allocation/staging/upload owner 校验，未留下兼容字段或 renderer-root reconstruction；per-page upload merge 的精确基数 guard 留在 `bitmap_run/tests/dirty_upload.rs`，没有把合并策略复制到 renderer root。当前实现状态为 `validation_pending`；expanded shared/SDF/raster-worker/default/UI/upward gates 仍由 Text 计划测试阶段拥有，不在本文复制或提前关闭。

当前文本图集基础设施同步（2026-08-24）：Text04 在最低 `text/atlas` owner 实现 persistent glyph slot MVP。`GlyphAtlasSet` 拥有 neutral `GlyphRasterKey -> page/generation/inserted-frame/rect/content-size` 与 per-page shelf allocator；后续帧精确命中复用 rect 并跳过 dirty/upload。mixed storage 现在保留一个 canonical frame submission，以 ordered resource segments 切换 bind group，而不是创建子提交、renderer-root 重建、兼容 facade 或双轨 API。页逐出、page generation 与 page-size 变化会 fail-closed 清理或拒绝旧 slot；裁剪、近似桶、pixel-font 和无稳定 face identity 不进入 persistent 模式。native glyphon `TextAtlas` 输入已移除；实现与静态门禁已完成，受管 Cargo、真实 WGPU framebuffer、GPU/功耗采样仍为 `validation_pending`。

当前文本 artifact identity 同步（2026-08-26）：公共 opaque handle 已删除 identity-free 构造器后，Text08 的 secure-presentation marker 仍有一处旧调用，会直接阻断 current-source 编译；其 layout child 同时从未导出该函数的 `ui::text` facade 导入，也会阻断编译。该 marker 现由 secure presentation owner 注册显式版本化 identity，layout child 直接依赖真实 sibling owner；独立分配保持 value equality，同时 payload 类型识别不变，没有恢复兼容构造器、增加 facade re-export 或把 identity 规则移入公共 DTO。focused regression、精确 leaf Rustfmt、旧构造器静态扫描和 793 行文件预算通过；状态为 `compile_repair_complete / managed_validation_pending`，不能据此声称 Cargo、WGPU、截图、性能或功耗验收完成。

当前插件架构审查同步（2026-07-10，`plugins_01_m2_t2_t4_typed_extension_freeze_runtime_finalize`）：Plugins 01 的类型化扩展点已补实际 runtime finalize/apply 闭环与 owner unload 回归；实现和测试均归属现有子模块/独立 test owner，没有新增 root 行为、兼容 facade 或 shim。详细证据归档在 Plugins 01 与 `docs/zircon_runtime/plugin/extension_registry.md`；插件架构整体状态：进行中，Sound 等后续计划不得据此标记完成。

> 本文是 2026-06-22 一轮聚焦代码审查的发现目录，作为结构规范与各子计划的**补充输入**。每条带 file:line 证据、严重度与目标计划。规范级规则已并入 [`engine-code-structure-convention.md`](engine-code-structure-convention.md)；结构级发现已并入 Runtime 15 / Editor UI 10 / Plugins 12；属既有受 guard 计划（02/04/06/07/08/09、render）的项在此登记为"建议补入"，由该计划 owner 在其切片内吸收（避免直接改动受计数 guard 的文件）。
>

## 1. P0 —— 健壮性 / 已知性能（优先排期）

> 请将产出记录放置在子计划中，子计划中记录超过10条则全部放到子目录，此处仅展示当前现状的概述

P0 项的闭合状态、验证、守卫与补记已迁入 Runtime 15 产出目录；此处只保留发现目录与承接计划。

| # | 发现概述 | 承接计划 |
|---|----------|----------|
| F1 | 原生插件入站 host 回调必须在跨 FFI 前截断 panic。 | Runtime 15 + Runtime 06 + Plugins 11 |
| F2 | scene/EventBus shared locks 需要 poison recovery，不能让 `.lock().unwrap()` 崩溃生产路径。 | Runtime 15 + Runtime 07 |
| F3 | 每帧渲染提交存在整帧 extract clone、相机循环 clone 与大 Vec 拷贝热点。 | Runtime 07 + render |
| F4 | render submit viewport/provider capability gap 必须返回 typed error，不应 panic。 | Runtime 07 + render |

2026-08-29 F4 当前状态：渲染提交的 advanced runtime plan 重复消费已改为
`RenderFrameworkError::InvalidSubmissionState`，SceneLinear phase 缺失已改为
`GraphicsError::MissingViewFamilyPhase`，并在 scene resource producer 已准备后沿既有 frame
transaction 失败结算路径返回；双级 spatial upscale 的非法 phase 与缺失输入也以
`UpscaleExecutionError` fail closed。后续同域复核又将 post-process graph execution 的
4 个直接 phase `expect` 与 3 个共享尺寸/phase-target `expect` 改为错误返回，14 个
GPU record 函数显式传播并合并一次重复 phase 查询。两个 render-submit 生产目录与 post-process graph execution
生产子域的 scoped `panic!`/`expect`/`unwrap` 扫描均为 0；preview-sky 的最后一个时间分离
context `expect` 随后也改为既有 `Result` 边界，使整个 `render_pass_execution_context` 生产子域
扫描为 0。扩大到完整 graph execution 后，pending transient allocation 缺失 ticket 改为
fail-closed 丢弃并计入既有 diagnostics，pool byte report 改为饱和转换，generic compute
nonzero range 与 texture-view format 改为单次模式匹配；排除明确 test-only 父模块后，完整
graph execution 生产候选扫描也为 0。编译场景资源绑定随后将 live scene-velocity 物理 backing
缺失改为 `GraphicsError::MissingFrameGraphResourceBacking`，prepared GPU Scene upload 缺失改为
`GraphicsError::MissingPreparedGpuSceneUpload`；后者在返回前先终止 pending realtime-IBL
submission。两个错误在 framework boundary 保留 typed identity，compiled/direct scene core 的
production-only 候选扫描均为 0。随后复核整个 `scene_renderer/core`，将 hit-proxy readback
聚合、cubemap upload target 索引和 scene-uniform packed upload 的 7 个生产 `expect` 全部改为
`let-else`、已有 `MissingTarget` 或 `InvalidBufferUploadRange` 错误；该目录 production-only
候选扫描仍为 0。精确格式、source guard、diff
与 Cargo metadata 静态检查通过。受管测试请求 `6d6bb68074fd4e3e8d6abaa787698b96`
在进入 Rust 编译前因 `cargo.acquire` 15 秒超时退出，故当前仅为源码实施，不能视为动态、
GPU 或性能验收。

随后复核 `render_graph` packet lowering：访问索引缺失返回
`CompiledAccessIndexEntryMissing`，dispatch 声明缺失返回 `ResourceDeclarationMissing`，空
binding 候选返回既有 `ComputeBindingAccessMissing`；render_graph 生产候选
`panic!`/`expect`/`unwrap` 扫描仍为 0。该切片仅收敛编译期错误终态，不改变访问候选选择、
范围匹配或 dispatch 计算算法。
同一 F4 复核还将 `ViewportSurface` 的 BGRA/RGBA blit pipeline 格式转换接入
`create_viewport_surface -> Result`，固定格式契约失败现在返回 `GraphicsError::SurfaceStatus`，
不再在生产构造器中 `expect`。
RGBA32F product diagnostic decoder 的定长 lane 转换也改为 `chunks_exact(4)`，保留严格 16-byte
输入校验和 little-endian 结果，不再依赖生产 `expect`。
内建 texture descriptor catalog 的缺失 phase/target 也改为 `Option` fail-closed，由既有
`missing_schema_error` `Result<String>` 边界报告，不再在 allocation extent 推导中 `panic!`。
viewport history 成功帧记录的最后一个 `(existing=None, allocated=None)` `unreachable!` 也已
改为 `RenderFrameworkError::InvalidSubmissionState`；capture、present、runtime-frame 与
non-viewport camera 路径显式传播，并在写入其它 viewport 状态前先完成 history 记录。
扩大到完整 `submit_frame_extract` 非测试生产域的 `panic!/expect/unwrap` 扫描为 0；动态
Cargo/WGPU 与产品帧验证仍待受管通道。

2026-08-30 F4 advanced-lighting follow-up：Froxel media-inject/light-scatter/integrate、planar-filter、subsurface pipeline cache 与 irradiance-volume 参数 upload 的生产初始化/范围异常均已改为 typed `Result` fail-closed；固定 dispatch 的无必要切片 `unwrap` 同时删除。compiled graph stage execution 还接入 immutable packet `begin/admit/finish` 游标，跨 stage 重排、遗漏或重复会在提交前报告 typed error。该同步只记录源码边界收敛，受管 Cargo、真实 WGPU、PNG/RDC、性能与功耗仍 pending。

2026-08-30 F4 render-resource ownership follow-up：post-process exposure、color-LUT bake 与 Hybrid-GI transient handoff 的 graph buffer consumer 已统一采用 compiler-proven `wgpu::BufferBinding`；histogram clear、scene packet upload 及 compute/render bind group 均受 exact offset/size 约束，persistent fallback 明确使用 full-range binding。该切片保持 shader/binding ABI 不变，只消除 non-compute transient whole-buffer 绕过；动态编译、WGPU、截图/RenderDoc、性能与功耗仍 pending。

2026-08-30 F4 execution ownership follow-up：compiled render packet 为 live pass 建立 O(1) batch ownership reverse index，stage-to-batch consumer 携带全局 batch identity，admission 在 frame-local cursor 之外再校验 pass 未跨 queue batch 路由。该索引只服务 immutable execution ownership，未扩大到 history/readback/barrier 或 external lease 语义；动态验证与性能证据仍 pending。

2026-08-30 F4 external lease follow-up：`CompiledRenderGraph` 为 live external access 生成 immutable exact-ID packet，frame resource table 在 physical binding 完成后建立 access-ID lease；generic-compute external texture/buffer/indirect dispatch 不再从 resource name 解析 WGPU 对象，buffer schema window 需与 lease window 完全相等。report-only descriptor-less imports仅保留兼容路径，需 pipeline descriptor 时 typed fail-closed；其余 non-compute external consumer、动态 WGPU、截图和性能/功耗仍 pending。

`CoreError`”硬切为最低资源 owner。`core::resource::{ResourceRegistryError,

`core` root 不增加 alias/re-export。该切片消除未来 `zr_resource -> zr_contracts` 的错误类型
反向边；静态守卫已补强旧变体、root 重导出与 conversion shim 的负向约束，独立复审
P0/P1/P2=0；managed Cargo 门仍按 Frameworks01 current-source 记录等待依赖提交与 FIFO 前置，本全局同步项不提前关闭 F6。

2026-08-30 F16 current-source同步：`render_compiled_scene()`已将frame foundation、mesh submission preparation、graph-frame preparation与accepted-frame success commit迁入具名child；编排root为481行。resource binding root为153行，SSAO params绑定在58行child；scene submit root为627行，HZB readback与submission-order tests分别由36/137行folder-backed owner承接。`review_f16_compiled_scene_render_path_uses_split_owners`继续锁定`<500/<160/<650`严格预算并新增这些owner锚点，未放宽既有门禁。该同步仅有rustfmt/source contract/locked metadata/scoped diff证据，managed Cargo与真实WGPU仍pending，因此F16保持validation-pending而非accepted。

2026-08-30 frame submission receipt current-source同步：P0-5新增逐帧logical packet、flush ticket、physical backend submit与upload workload合同后，原515行receipt root不能继续吸收测试。统计DTO、typed boundary reason与producer record分别成为93/12/90行owner；完整receipt测试迁入203行folder-backed child，production receipt root当前334行。transaction root/tests为174/228行，Runtime09A结构守卫root/child为351/122行。backend负责WGPU单调delta映射，core不依赖WGPU snapshot；纹理具体variant只在resource streamer赋值，producer/reason错误配对在transaction修改前返回typed error并由receipt再次校验。精确rustfmt、采样/边界顺序、success/failure reason传播、错误配对拒绝、无额外flush/poll与locked metadata静态证据通过，managed Cargo和真实WGPU profile仍pending；最新状态`runtime09a_texture_mip_preservation_typed_boundary_source_implemented_static_checks_passed_dynamic_validation_pending`。

2026-08-30 hit-proxy submission transaction current-source同步：独立hit-proxy task原先在bounded diagnostic admission/prepare之前enqueue GPU-scene upload并立即commit CPU状态，预算拒绝或prepare失败可留下由后续无关submission flush的孤儿upload。当前顺序已硬切为diagnostic prepare→upload enqueue→terminal command+diagnostic submit→GPU-scene commit；所有diagnostic fallible边界发生在upload admission前，commit只在terminal submit成功路径。production enqueue/commit各一个调用点，516行owner内源码回归、精确rustfmt与scoped diff通过。managed Cargo、真实WGPU/device-loss/pick像素和profile仍pending；该修复不代表独立task已纳入统一receipt，状态`runtime09a_hit_proxy_submission_transaction_order_source_implemented_static_checks_passed_dynamic_validation_pending`。

2026-08-30 P0-6 readback current-source复审：旧评审中“多个产品buffer/texture/IBL helper逐次submit并`wait_indefinitely`”已不再成立；这些owner当前由module-level `#[cfg(test)]`隔离。产品viewport/HDR capture、hit-proxy和IBL/query diagnostics走有界generation/ticket service，正常scene与viewport-pick复用唯一completion fan-out；显式capture有30秒deadline且每次poll receipt仍路由全部consumer。普通产品路径唯一`wait_indefinitely`是RenderDoc/Xcode stop控制边界。仍开放standalone native UI独立device/raw present submit与本地timeline、显式capture consumer executor/cancel policy、真实fault/scale/profile验收。状态`runtime09a_product_readback_current_source_reaudit_documented_remaining_boundaries_open`，不得继续按旧helper拓扑实施优化。

## 4. 正面基线（防回退输入）

- `zircon_runtime_interface` 边界应保持轻依赖和 ABI-safe DTO。
- editor/app 的窗口与事件循环依赖边界应保持清晰，不把 graphics backend 泄入 authoring-only 层。

## 5. 优先级建议

P0 安全与崩溃类、渲染性能热点、插件 DX 单源化与结构门禁继续由对应子计划维护；本文件只作为发现目录和分派入口。

## 2026-07-10 Runtime Text UAX#9 convergence follow-up

- The text layout visual-order structure finding stays closed while its implementation is strengthened: `ui/text/layout_engine/visual_order.rs` remains a child owner, but delegates Unicode paragraph/line authority to `text/shaping/bidi.rs`.
- This removes three duplicated policy surfaces from UI layout—ASCII-only first-strong detection, handcrafted neutral-span ordering, and a second mirror table—without widening the root module or preserving a compatibility shim.
- Wrapped-line analysis consumes full paragraph text plus absolute source ranges so isolate embedding context is not discarded at line boundaries; exact-owner tests cover this boundary.

## 2026-07-10 Runtime Text backend face-ID reconciliation follow-up

- Text 06 D4 is hard-cut from post-shape script/codepoint recomputation to actual backend identity: `text/font/{backend.rs,database.rs,shared.rs}` owns the database lineage and ID reconciliation, and cosmic/native consume `LayoutGlyph.font_id` directly.
- The former `text/shaping/font_id.rs` bridge and its unused resolver API are deleted rather than hidden with `allow(dead_code)` or retained as compatibility surface.
- `font/shared.rs` uses poison recovery for its production `RwLock` and a generation snapshot, satisfying E9 while avoiding a global lock on the steady-state shaping hot path.
- Detailed status and validation remain owned by `docs/plans/zircon_runtime/text/06-font-fallback.md`; bounded missing-glyph diagnostics and resolver-side partial-cluster handling are closed. Historical runtime WGPU evidence covered CJK, Arabic, Hebrew, mixed BiDi, native color emoji, SDF, and zh-Hans/ja same-codepoint divergence, but it is not current-source acceptance; a fresh managed product framebuffer remains required. The selected `Segoe UI Emoji` backend face is directly verified as `SwashContent::Color` with RGBA-sized bytes; after the monolithic lib-test timed out, the isolated executable closed the same complex-cluster invariant by proving Arabic base+fatha produced two glyphs on one actual `Segoe UI` face.
- The follow-up keeps report growth folder-backed: missing-glyph frame-out aggregation is in `scene_renderer/ui/text/prepare_report.rs`, leaving the renderer root below the 800-line soft budget and avoiding another root-level diagnostics DTO block.

## 2026-07-10 Runtime Text locale-cache review follow-up (2026-08-26 current-source update)

- `UiResolvedStyle.language` remains a neutral serialized string, but normalization policy no longer lives in `zircon_runtime_interface`: `text/language.rs` is the sole Runtime Text owner. ICU4X `Locale::normalize` now validates syntax and emits canonical language/script/region casing; underscore input is accepted only at the application boundary. Invalid non-empty tags become `TextLayoutError::InvalidLanguage` before backend or shaped-cache use.
- `CanonicalBackendShapeRequest` owns a changed canonical tag and marks its private reborrow as canonical. The normal `layout_session -> service -> shape_text` chain therefore parses at most once; an already canonical tag stays borrowed and does not allocate an output string. The shaped cache hashes and compares exact canonical identity, deleting its former byte-by-byte lowercase/separator policy.
- The bounded four-entry Cosmic `FontSystem` cache trusts the canonical request and allocates a locale string only on a locale-cache miss. Template/layout/native/SDF consumers call the same Runtime owner; the former interface helper and cache-local normalization functions are deleted rather than retained as aliases.
- This boundary follows Unreal's `FCulture` structure: canonical name plus script/region are owned centrally, while prioritized parent-culture combinations remain a separate fallback decision. Zircon has completed only canonical tag identity; likely-subtag expansion, explicit script/region output, versioned locale data and fallback decision receipt remain open under `RTS-P1-002`.
- Static formatting, whitespace, retired-helper and call-site scans pass. Managed Cargo, locale corpus, cache-cardinality/timing, RSS/power, WGPU and screenshot evidence are still pending, so the status is `canonical_tag_non_validation_implemented / likely_subtag_receipt_open / managed_validation_pending`, not acceptance.

## 2026-08-26 Runtime Text Unicode-data identity follow-up

- Runtime Text no longer labels all Unicode behavior with one false aggregate version. `text/unicode_data.rs` records nine independently pinned providers: locale parser `icu_locale_core 2.2.0`; Normalization 17; Bidi/Mirroring 16; Script/Grapheme/Emoji 17; LineBreak 15; and VerticalOrientation revision 17. Provider implementation revisions and data versions feed one stable schema fingerprint.
- The complete descriptor has one owner; hot paths carry only a 16-byte `UnicodeDataSnapshotId { generation, fingerprint }`. The identity is frozen on `BackendShapeRequest`, retained by script/emoji analysis, Bidi paragraph/line artifacts, the line-break opportunity map and `ShapedGlyphRun`, participates in every exact and direction-alias shaped-cache fingerprint, and is published by `TextLayoutFallbackReport`. Direct/Cosmic consumers and cache insertion assert request/artifact or key/artifact identity equality.
- The Unicode/locale dependencies are exact-version pinned to the current lockfile. An upgrade must update the descriptor and generation deliberately. Serialized shaped runs require the identity; missing legacy identity fails deserialization instead of being filled with the current provider identity, so old artifacts cannot silently masquerade as new-data output.
- This is `compiled_snapshot_identity_implemented / static_checks_complete / managed_validation_pending`. Dynamic provider replacement, old-generation lease retirement, full analysis/layout/document artifact propagation, managed tests, performance/power evidence and render screenshots remain open; `RTS-GATE-003/012` are not closed.

## 2026-08-26 Runtime Text shaping-failure policy follow-up

- Direct shaping failures now cross one typed policy owner instead of a Bidi boolean plus a catch-all. `shaping/failure_receipt.rs` maps itemization, Bidi, backend, face, source-range and glyph-invariant errors to 12 explicit stable codes with phase, range, face, dependency, disposition and optional budget.
- The low-cardinality report uses a fixed `[u64; 12]` counter and stores the last Copy receipt; its mutex is acquired only on a direct failure, never in the successful glyph/cluster loop. The retired `is_bidi_invariant` classifier count is zero and the production failure-policy owner count is one.
- Cosmic fallback is now admitted only for horizontal backend/font-face/glyph capability failures. Bidi, invalid source ranges, itemization/fallback-span inconsistencies and `FontDatabaseError::SourceBudget` fail closed, so fallback cannot hide structural corruption or bypass font admission budgets. Vertical direct failures remain terminal.
- All non-Ready `TextShapingOutcome` branches now carry one internal `TextShapingFailure { error, receipt }`. Direct terminal failures and failures after an admitted alternate-backend attempt retain the request receipt across the stable font-generation boundary, session/cache owner, layout transforms and internal UI forwarding; `map`/`and_then` preserve it and non-Ready outcomes remain inadmissible to the shaped cache. The process-global report is diagnostic only and is never read to decide a request.
- The public `TextLayoutService` boundary deliberately projects only the existing neutral `TextLayoutError`, avoiding a `core/framework/text -> text` reverse dependency on implementation-owned face/range types. This follows Unreal's shaped-sequence/source-range ownership while adding the typed failure contract absent from its reference-returning Slate shaper API. The later 2026-08-26 M0-M2 record below supersedes the former whole-request-only horizontal recovery status; managed validation remains open.

## 2026-07-10 Runtime Text vertical owner review follow-up

- The previous all-vertical-glyphs-Cw90 shortcut is removed. Unicode Vertical_Orientation is now the single Mixed-mode authority in `text/shaping/vertical/orientation.rs`, with CJK/fullwidth upright and Latin sideways behavior covered by exact leaf tests.
- VerticalRl layout now routes wrapping, ellipsis, and measurement through a vertical shaped-run provider/cache key; the UI layout child no longer silently asks for horizontal shaping data.
- Structure remains folder-backed and bounded: orientation rules and their tests are children, the root shaping module only wires providers, and no old horizontal-only alias is retained as a success path.
- NFC remains intentionally disabled in V1, but the old implicit assumption is removed: `normalize.rs::ShapingTextView` now owns the identity view/source projection and exact tests lock decomposed byte preservation. Any future NFC feature must extend that owner with bidirectional mapping instead of rewriting contract ranges in `cosmic.rs`.
- LB-M4 now closes the review-plan's first vertical ownership cut: column capacity, right-to-left frame placement, and axis extents are no longer authored in `ui/text/layout_engine/vertical.rs`; they are exact-tested in `text/layout/vertical_layout.rs` and consumed by the UI projection adapter.
- Product-level TDD first exposed the render defect: VerticalRl SDF batches reused horizontal resolved advances and never rotated sideways UVs, collapsing all glyphs into one strip. The SDF child consumes the shared orientation/advance owner, swaps destination axes, rotates sideways UVs clockwise, and owns vertical main-axis advances. A subsequent CJK product gate exposed a lower shared-font defect: `StoredFontSource::FontDb` faces had backend IDs but `face_bytes(...)` always rejected them. `FontDatabase` now materializes bytes through the authoritative backend database and existing standalone-face extractor; the temporary coverage→SDF strategy was deleted. Native `vmtx` remains a font child owner. The current follow-up adds rustybuzz TTB/BTT plus backend vertical-origin offsets, preserves actual glyph/face identity through `render/text_advances.rs` and `sdf_atlas/text_keys.rs`, and bakes indexed glyphs on the authoritative face. A historical WGPU frame showed two right-to-left CJK columns with vertical punctuation, but it is not current-source acceptance; fresh managed WGPU output remains required. Soft-wrap affinity and VerticalRl layout/hit-test/IME filters are covered by their focused owners; horizontal per-run `locl` and variable axes now use the canonical RustyBuzz direct path, while mixed-BiDi product geometry and platform IME live QA remain explicit gaps.
- LB-M3 hit testing now consumes per-visual-cluster source ranges and direction through a folder-backed leaf, returning logical offset plus affinity for RTL edges. The old descending-range merge was deleted rather than hidden behind a fallback alias.

## 2026-07-11 Runtime Text mixed-storage ordering review follow-up

- Native bitmap atlas handoff no longer treats repeated storage formats as an unsupported mixed frame. `text/native_bitmap_atlas/storage.rs` partitions contiguous source runs, preserving the renderer's original glyph draw order for `R8 -> RGBA -> R8` instead of globally merging both R8 groups.
- The existing renderer pass sequence is the only execution owner; no compatibility sorter, second atlas route, or root-level special case was added. Current-source native atlas and renderer filters pass 44/44 and 13/13.
- This closes the historical ordering/fallback defect only. Persistent slot ownership and its native DPI key propagation contract are now `implemented / validation_pending` in Text04; the 2026-08-24 hard cut has removed native glyphon `TextAtlas` input in favor of canonical shaped glyph projection. Real 2x WGPU framebuffer product evidence, managed validation, and live editor typography QA remain explicitly open.
- 2026-08-08 Text04 DPI input closure: the serialized `UiRenderExtract.raster_scale` now carries the retained window scale through full rebuild, geometry patch, render-cache reuse, plain/rich/inline batch planning, and the native `GlyphRasterKey`; invalid or legacy values normalize to `1.0`. The logical layout/shaping cache and SDF/MSDF route remain logical, while the native raster key receives physical glyph sizes. Secondary review repaired and covered the native-fallback overlay propagation, then reported no P0/P1/P2. To retain the structure budget, `surface/rebuild.rs` now only orchestrates rebuilds (715 lines); pipeline-report diagnostics live in `surface/rebuild/report.rs` (351 lines). Managed Cargo, real 2x WGPU framebuffer evidence, and the required product PNG remain pending and are not claimed here.
- 2026-08-08 Text04 DPI product-gate forward repair: the first 2x WGPU gate scaled both viewport/frame, so bounds and coverage could pass after a stale 1x bitmap enlargement. It now retains one WGPU renderer and asset runtime, submits the same device-space frame and text at 1x then 2x, and requires the second phase to expose an exact native source-cache miss through `RenderStats` before comparing framebuffer geometry and coverage. The post-repair second source review found no P0/P1/P2. This is source-level verification only: the ignored WGPU gate, managed Cargo, and the required real PNG remain pending; no visual sharpness acceptance is claimed.
- 2026-08-24 Text04 canonical-frame follow-up: the earlier contiguous-format partition is historical, not the current execution contract. Native bitmap submission now has one frame owner for the atlas snapshot, source images, upload plan, and shadow commit. Painter order remains immutable as ordered draw segments, while upload targets are selected by page format from a fixed resource table. The table preflights every binding before the first queue write, rejects missing or duplicate format resources as a complete-frame failure, and then writes directly without per-resource temporary binding vectors. This removes the old `O(R * G)` per-segment plan rebuild and `O(F * B)` resource filtering paths without globally regrouping Alpha/Color/Alpha draws.
- Focused source regressions cover missing and duplicate resources; scoped formatting, diff, and retired-dispatch scans pass. Managed Cargo, 1/100/1k/10k timings, GPU timestamps, RenderDoc, power, real WGPU framebuffer capture, and a fresh PNG remain pending, so this update is `implementation_complete / managed_validation_pending`, not a Text04 acceptance or commit.

## 2026-07-31 Runtime Text rich layout performance/structure review follow-up

- The rich horizontal/VerticalRl wrap paths no longer perform provider shaping per grapheme. `text/layout/advance_index.rs` owns shared sorted grapheme metrics plus prefix advances, while `rich_advance_index.rs` compiles continuous style/inline spans into it; both axes and UI plain wrapping consume the same range-query owner.
- Per-line full-source cloning and all-run filtering were removed. `text/layout/rich/materialize.rs` borrows the canonical source, advances a monotonic run cursor, preserves original run indices, and projects line-item advances from the same index.
- UI Word/Glyph greedy wrapping now accumulates indexed chunk/grapheme advances and applies bounded line-edge corrections. The old growing-prefix String and complete-prefix reshape APIs were removed; scale regressions cover 1/100/1k/10k graphemes and bounded shaped-cache misses.
- Secondary review found and fixed three actionable implementation defects before acceptance: duplicated plain/rich boundary planners, O(G^2) complete-candidate `BoundaryAdvanceUnit` collection despite bounded shaping, and rich-inline soft-hyphen width correction without synthetic suffix projection. The final owner reads prefix raw advances and materializes at most 32 edge units; horizontal/VerticalRl rich UI append the same measured `-` suffix as the plain path.
- The structure finding remains closed under the current repository budget: `rich.rs` is 279 lines; `materialize.rs`, `advance_index.rs`, `rich_advance_index.rs`, and `metrics.rs` are 258/193/399/77; `rich_vertical.rs` is 232 and `boundary_correction.rs` is 358. UI `wrapping.rs`, `rich_inline.rs`, and `rich_inline_vertical.rs` are 417/315/273. Every owner remains below the 800-line production review warning. No production panic/unwrap/expect/dead-code allow, compatibility facade, second shaping backend, or scalar-width approximation was introduced.
- After those forward fixes, the secondary static review finds no remaining actionable P0/P1/P2 in the slice. It does not accept runtime behavior yet: managed Cargo, run-scale/p50/p95 evidence, Text09 cache regressions, and fresh WGPU product evidence remain owned by the open Text03 failure.

## 2026-08-24 Runtime Text outcome and intrinsic-measure follow-up

- The shaped-run cache owner and M2b provider path now classify canonical results as `Ready`, `Deferred`, or `Failed`; `SharedTextLayoutSession` and `text/parallel/shape_pool` admit only `Ready` through `ShapedRunCache::insert_ready`. A failed or generation-deferred request does not materialize an empty run and cannot poison a later cache hit. The parallel report separately exposes stable failures from generation deferrals, without adding a cache, facade, or renderer-side shaper.
- `TextShapeRunProvider`, measure/advance, line-break/boundary correction, rich/VerticalRl materialization, table cell layout, and UI safe publication have been migrated as one typed path. `UiTextMeasureCache` admits only a current-generation `Ready` resolution; `Deferred`/`Failed` publishes the overflow-clipped safe layout outside both frame and persistent caches. No compatibility provider or empty glyph-run fallback remains in this path.
- Rich and VerticalRl intrinsic measurement no longer constructs a square frame from UTF-8 byte length times `em`. The `ui/text/layout_engine.rs` helper uses Text03's existing positive-infinity unbounded-main-axis contract, forces non-painting measurement to logical start alignment, and derives VerticalRl's finite cross axis from canonical hard lines and the existing column advance. This avoids both byte/advance conflation and the vertical-layout finite sanitization of infinite width.
- Scope review found no new cross-crate text contract, no extra hot-path allocation for successful cached shaping, and all affected production owners remain below the 800-line review threshold. `rustfmt --check`, scoped whitespace checks, and static admission/retired-extent scans passed. Managed Cargo, fault injection, 1/100/1k/10k profiler and power captures, real WGPU framebuffer verification, fresh PNG output, milestone commit, and WeCom synchronization remain pending coordinator-owned evidence; none is claimed by this source review.
- Current-source static gates find no production panic/unwrap/expect/dead-code allow or deleted flat-atlas owner, all touched production owners stay below 800 lines, every exact `SdfAtlasBakeReport` constructor contains the compiled-atlas fields, focused rustfmt check and full `git diff --check` pass. The second static review finds no remaining actionable P0/P1/P2 in the implementation slice.
- Text05 failures remain `open / resolving_failure / non_validation_implementation_complete / secondary_review_complete / managed_validation_pending`; this review does not claim acceptance. Managed Cargo, 1/100/10k p50/p95/RSS, device loss, real WGPU/RenderDoc, and a fresh real-rendered PNG under `docs/tests/runtime/text` remain coordinator validation follow-ups; no policy-text screenshot is accepted.

- 2026-08-24 continuation: paragraph prewarm has hard-cut its obsolete `runs: Vec<Arc<ShapedGlyphRun>>` result. It is a cache-admission stage, not a layout provider: cache hits remain local, only `Ready` inserts an owned shaped run, and `Deferred`/`Failed` are counted without publishing an empty run. The per-request duplicate output index is removed with that surface, so the successful path also stops retaining an unnecessary result vector. UI frame aggregation and render profiling now project invalid, generation-deferred, and failed prewarm counts; regressions lock invalid aggregation, non-publication of deferred/failed/missing outcomes, and zero-valued empty-stage counters.
- P1-13 M2a and its M2b typed-consumer hard cut are implementation-complete: the explicit failure regression confirms an invalid shaping request produces no frame/persistent cache entry, no glyph artifact, and no published text line. This does not claim managed Cargo, WGPU capture, screenshot, milestone acceptance, commit, or WeCom synchronization.
- M2b cache publication remains aligned with the real key contract: `UiTextMeasureKey` contains font-database generation, so a previous-generation ready entry is intentionally stale rather than a same-key deferred fallback. The implemented hard cut admits only exact current-generation Ready values; Deferred/Failed publishes the overflow-clipped safe layout without frame/persistent cache admission. This matches Unreal `FShapedTextCache::FindShapedText`, whose stale shaped text is a miss, and avoids inventing a second stale-layout cache.
- 2026-08-24 artifact-boundary follow-up: the previously untyped glyph-artifact `Option` return could erase an internal shaping failure and let the outer plain-layout path publish `Ready(layout)` without an artifact. Ordinary and secure-presentation artifact builders now return `TextLayoutOutcome<Option<...>>`; valid DTO-only cases remain `Ready(None)`, while failed shaping and a font-generation change respectively remain `Failed` and `Deferred` through the sole layout publication owner. The attachment protocol lives in the focused `ui/text/layout_engine/artifact.rs` child rather than extending the 777-line layout root, and render-command preparation applies the same outcome policy to secure masks. Ellipsis/virtual/presentation paths that deliberately retain only visual DTO data remain eligible for `Ready(None)` and keep the existing fail-closed renderer fallback. No public DTO, renderer-local source mapper, second shaper, compatibility facade, or new cache was added.
- Unreal Slate's shared shaped-sequence path rejects dirty cache values and requires a valid final subsequence; Fyrox keeps source, line ranges, and glyph output under one formatted-text owner. Zircon deliberately preserves its stronger typed failure boundary. Four focused cases live in `glyph_artifact/tests/invariant_failures.rs`; the production root remains 797 lines and the 969-line test root does not grow further. Formatting, whitespace, call-path, and file-budget checks pass as static evidence only. `RTS-P1-047/RTS-GATE-044`, managed Cargo, profiler/power capture, current WGPU framebuffer under `docs/tests/runtime/text`, screenshot inspection, commit, and WeCom synchronization remain open.

- The Runtime UI owner bypass identified in RTS-P1-046 is structurally corrected: full and

  `measure_node_incremental`, fixed-width leaf measurement, and ordinary leaf measurement now

  only the separately admitted parallel collection phase can defer owner text before the join.

- Static closeout corrected the remaining tests that named the deleted renderer line rebuild and

## 2026-08-26 Runtime Text script itemization correction

- Review found three coupled defects in one owner boundary: script runs ignored Script_Extensions,
  paired brackets were only sticky Common characters, and fallback encoded the cluster's first
  codepoint as `FontScript::Other`. Fallback and HarfBuzz could therefore choose different script
  identities while the cache cardinality grew by character instead of script.
- The paragraph script owner now intersects `unicode-script` fixed-size extension sets and resolves
  brackets through `unicode-bidi` BidiBrackets data. Opening context, matching close inheritance,
  leading/nested brackets, explicit BCP-47 script subtags, and neighbor disambiguation feed one
  segment set shared by fallback, horizontal/vertical direct shaping, and Cosmic projection.
- `FontScript::Other(FontScriptTag)` now means a validated canonical-form packed ISO15924 tag;
  private storage plus checked deserialization prevent arbitrary integers from re-entering the asset
  schema while preserving its existing numeric Serde shape. Unassigned characters use `Unknown`.
  The old handwritten font script ranges, first-codepoint script path, and raw `Other(u32)` calls are
  gone. The unresolved-bracket
  suffix ensures each opening is filled once, while matching close search pops discarded nesting,
  preserving amortized linear stack work without a global cache or per-character set allocation.
- Focused regressions and scoped formatter/whitespace/call-site/file-budget scans are present. This
  is non-acceptance evidence only: managed Cargo, official Unicode corpus, cache cardinality and
  latency/RSS/power measurements, real WGPU output, PNG inspection under `docs/tests/runtime/text`,
  milestone commit, and WeCom synchronization remain pending.

  comparison-shape optimization remain open under `RTS-P1-013/018/019/021`.

  BackendShapeError>`, share a 38-line error owner, preserve each font database source, and distinguish
  face parse from empty output. A focused unknown-face contract retains operation, requested face, and
  `FontDatabaseError::UnknownFace`.
- The direct layer hard cut is now present as well. `DirectShapeError` retains itemization, Bidi,
  backend, invalid-source-range, and backend-glyph invariant failures; the latter distinguishes empty
  output, invalid UTF-8 cluster offsets, non-finite metrics, and non-monotonic cluster order. Horizontal
  and vertical direct shaping return a required run or that receipt, never `Option`.

  `TextLayoutError::BidiInvariant`; every other direct failure follows the existing orientation policy
  (horizontal may use whole-request Cosmic fallback, vertical remains `ShapingFailed`). This preserves
  product behavior while keeping the exact cause alive until that boundary. Static scans find 0 backend

  scoped formatter, whitespace, call-site, and file-budget checks pass.
- The adjacent boundary review also removed two representation holes. An invalid hard-line separator
  range is now a typed itemization source-range failure rather than `Ok(None)`, while the RustyBuzz
  adapters consume validated `Iso15924Tag` values directly instead of degrading them to `&str` and
  reparsing with `.ok()?`. Common/Inherited/Unknown still deliberately leave script inference to the
  backend; this is policy, not a parse failure.
- The classification and invalid-cluster/itemization regressions are source-present but unexecuted.
  Fault injection, low-cardinality failure reporting, capability-versus-invariant policy expansion,
  managed Cargo, timing/power, WGPU/PNG, milestone commit, and WeCom synchronization remain open. This
  is `typed_direct_receipt_implemented / static_checks_complete / managed_validation_pending`, not an

## 2026-08-26 Runtime Text ligature caret ownership finding

- Current layout measurement divides a multi-grapheme backend cluster advance evenly among its
  graphemes, while the retained glyph artifact hit path already treats the same cluster atomically.
  Missing or stale artifact fallback can therefore publish a different, falsely precise caret model.
- Unreal retains character and grapheme-cluster counts on each shaped entry and treats entries with
  multiple grapheme clusters as atomic for offset lookup and overflow clipping. It does not manufacture
  midpoint carets from equal advance shares.
- The local `ttf-parser 0.21.1/0.25.1` sources skip GDEF LigCaretList, and RustyBuzz 0.20.1 exposes no
  ligature-caret API. Zircon cannot honestly label the current fallback font-derived.

  `AtomicCluster` policy. This is `architecture_review_complete / implementation_open`; `RTS-P1-034`,
  managed tests, corpus, performance/power, WGPU/PNG, commit, and WeCom remain open.

## 2026-08-26 Runtime Text rich artifact ownership correction

- **Structural defect found:** `UiResolvedTextLayout.rich_text_artifact` was used as an exclusive
  exact-downcast slot for either compiled rich metadata or resolved glyphs. Keeping links and inline
  metadata therefore forced the renderer to reshape each visual paint run; RTL projection could turn
  one logical joining run into several grapheme-sized shaping requests.
- **Implemented owner correction:** `text/runtime_artifact.rs` privately composes compiled metadata,
  one immutable line glyph artifact, the exact layout snapshot, and a run-slice directory under the
  existing public handle. Layout and render preparation publish that owner; renderer extraction only
  resolves and validates slices. Public DTOs and neutral framework contracts do not acquire renderer
  types.
- **Duplicate-work guard:** a glyph is owned once even when it spans style runs. Continuation runs
  receive an empty slice receipt, while intentional fallback lines retain a negative receipt. Cache
  identity includes glyph slice bounds, preventing different run views from aliasing one render key.
- **Performance boundary:** construction is a monotonic
  `O(line/style intersections + glyphs + clusters + runs)` pass with one font-pair registration batch
  per physical line. Profiling counters are aggregated per owner, never per glyph/grapheme. Managed
  Cargo, scale profiles, RSS/power, Unreal comparison and framebuffer evidence remain pending; no
  performance acceptance or bottleneck-removal claim is recorded.
- **Horizontal U+00AD follow-up:** the break hint is consumed in source space while the display run
  uses a zero-width end anchor. The request-local logical-display sidecar owns UAX#9 order; a monotonic
  cluster-to-source-style pass coalesces equal rich styles before shaping and publishes one virtual
  glyph slice. Font-generation rebuild may reuse the sidecar only when source, style, writing mode and
  exact layout-line snapshot still match. VerticalRl now consumes the same display-owned decision
  through an explicit `DiscretionaryHyphen` role and retained logical sidecar.
- Status: `rich_composite_artifact_and_run_slice_route_implemented /
  rich_horizontal_soft_hyphen_virtual_artifact_implemented / static_checks_complete /
  managed_profile_pending`.

## 2026-08-26 Runtime Text rich virtual-style ownership correction

- **Structural defect found:** a generated rich run published a zero-width source anchor, while both
  artifact shaping and renderer presentation tried to infer style from that anchor. Start ellipsis at
  a style boundary could fail artifact publication; any virtual marker could paint with base font,
  color, or decoration even when its glyph was shaped by a rich run.
- **Implemented owner correction:** request-local `CandidateLine` now assigns every generated cluster
  a non-empty style-source owner. The immutable logical sidecar includes that receipt in rebuild
  identity; the glyph directory returns it with the zero-width slice, and renderer presentation uses
  the same compiled rich run. Public UI DTOs remain unchanged.
- **Ordering correction:** horizontal text-only rich ellipsis is created in logical order before
  UAX#9. End/EndWord/Middle use the preceding current run; Start uses the following run. That owner
  supplies marker measurement as well as artifact and paint style. Horizontal soft hyphen supplies
  its consumed U+00AD range through the same contract.
- **Omitted-source defect found:** the public zero-width source anchor described only the insertion
  point, so caret, hit-test, and selection geometry could not recover the source interval replaced by
  an ellipsis. The candidate/logical/glyph receipt now carries an optional replaced-source range and
  publishes it only when the retained-range complement is exactly one non-empty interval; ambiguous
  gaps fail closed. The private glyph owner maps caret affinity, marker midpoint hits, and selection
  coverage without changing public UI DTOs.
- **Accessibility ownership confirmed:** accessibility name/value extraction reads original template
  metadata, component state, or widget value. It does not consume `UiResolvedTextLine.text`, so the
  visual marker and omitted-source geometry receipt cannot replace the semantic source string.
- **Complexity boundary:** owner selection, logical capture, style coalescing, glyph projection, and
  run publication are monotonic linear passes; geometry queries remain `O(clusters + glyphs)` and add
  no persistent paragraph-sized map. No style-by-grapheme nested scan or renderer-local paragraph
  reconstruction was added. This is static evidence only.
- **Inline-object structural defect fixed:** rich inline images/widgets were represented by compiled
  U+FFFC clusters for layout, but ordinary lines could still send that placeholder through font
  shaping. The layout owner now admits an explicit external-cluster receipt from the compiled inline
  source range even without ellipsis. UAX#9 and final advances retain the block, rich style spans split
  around it, glyph projection excludes it, and its paint run publishes an empty glyph slice. Literal
  U+FFFC is unaffected. Inline-only horizontal lines use the same receipt and may own a zero-glyph text
  artifact; this is not a negative/fallback artifact.
- **Geometry/structure correction:** external blocks and ellipsis replacement receipts share one
  visual-cluster geometry owner. A sidecar rejected by BiDi projection is now fail-closed for caret,
  hit-test, and selection as well as glyph publication. The 847-line glyph root was split into a
  538-line artifact owner and 361-line geometry leaf. The logical sidecar delegates monotonic glyph
  fragment caching, glyph projection, and receipt validation to 50/152/59-line leaves, leaving the
  owner at 762 lines; the 523-line rich builder also remains below the review threshold. Rich builder regressions
  live in a 327-line child.
- **Vertical capability gate:** the rich builder no longer rejects every VerticalRl line. Ordinary
  styled spans now call the same canonical vertical shaping provider used by Plain, and VerticalRl
  inline external blocks retain object advances while publishing empty glyph slices. Vertical rich
  now materializes ellipsis and discretionary hyphen before UAX#9, retains the logical sequence, and
  dispatches both typed roles to the vertical provider. Exact display-grapheme and replaced-U+00AD
  checks reject untyped or mismatched markers.
- **Virtual-receipt complexity defect fixed:** capture previously called `.iter().find` for every
  grapheme, giving `O(clusters * virtual receipts)` with many justification/generated fragments. It
  now validates ordered, non-overlapping receipts once and advances one monotonic cursor beside the
  existing external-range cursor, giving `O(clusters + virtual receipts + external ranges)`.
- **Renderer directory defect fixed:** the prior renderer called a layout-line `.find` and then an
  artifact-run `.find` for every rich paint run, so a line with `R` styled runs had worst-case
  `O(R^2)` publication before shaping/paint. Paint runs are already emitted in exact flattened layout
  order; publication now walks layout lines/runs once and uses an exact O(1) directory index resolver,
  giving `O(lines + runs)`. Text/range/order mismatch rejects the whole route batch as incomplete.
- **Typed rich route receipt:** rich publication no longer collapses intentional negative artifacts,
  absent handles, stale font/layout snapshots, invalid slice bounds, and missing directory entries to
  `Option::None`. `Artifact`, `VisualOnly`, and `Missing/Stale/Incomplete` counts enter the existing
  low-cardinality route report and profiling aggregates. The normal profiling regression now requires
  canonical rich runs and zero renderer fallback-shape spans. Rejected runs only retain the existing
  renderer fallback when the resolved line proves exact source isomorphism; generated/non-isomorphic
  missing or stale runs are dropped rather than reshaped from guessed visual text.
- **Owner budget:** rich planning moved out of the 816-line renderer root. Current production sizes are
  754 (`render.rs`), 485 (`rich_text.rs`), 727 (`resolved_layout.rs`), and 339
  (`runtime_artifact.rs`); route tests moved from the old 855-line root into a 384-line child, leaving
  the test root at 651 lines.
- Status: `rich_text_only_ellipsis_virtual_artifact_implemented /
  virtual_source_and_style_receipt_implemented /
  private_omitted_source_geometry_receipt_implemented /
  horizontal_inline_external_cluster_artifact_implemented /
  inline_empty_glyph_slice_and_geometry_receipt_implemented /
  vertical_rich_and_external_block_canonical_artifact_implemented /
  vertical_rich_ellipsis_virtual_artifact_implemented /
  vertical_rich_soft_hyphen_virtual_artifact_implemented /

## 2026-08-26 Runtime Text dynamic inline widget architecture correction

- **Historical structural defect:** `[widget]` carried only bare `u64 + size`; layout reserved an external
  block, while the graphics rich renderer painted a solid rectangle. No real UI child participated in
  lifetime, arrange, render extract, input, focus, or accessibility, so the old output is not an
  Unreal-equivalent widget run.
- **Reference boundary:** local Unreal `FSlateWidgetRun` strongly owns `SWidget`, measures explicit or
  desired size, arranges it as a child, calls its normal `Paint`, and dirties only the run when desired
  size changes. Zircon must preserve that child-owner boundary rather than create a renderer registry.
- **Selected MVP contract:** authored `widthxheight` is the explicit run size; `id` binds one direct
  child of the rich-text UI owner. The UI tree remains the lifetime/input/focus/a11y/paint owner and
  text layout publishes only canonical absolute placement. Invalid, duplicate, cross-parent, or
  overflow-omitted bindings fail closed and cannot retain stale hit geometry. The renderer placeholder
  has been deleted; the real child now reaches the ordinary render-extract and hit-test paths.
- **Implemented path:** compiled widget source ranges are projected through canonical resolved
  lines/runs to absolute frames. Full layout scans all roots; incremental layout scans only the current
  arrangement-root subtrees. Duplicate, missing, cross-parent, and omitted bindings clear child
  subtree geometry instead of preserving stale layout.
- **Identity correction:** compiled artifacts now retain `RichInlineWidgetSlotId`, an authoring-local
  slot rather than a live `UiNodeId`. UI text projection is tree-identity-free; Surface layout resolves
  the slot only against the current owner's direct children while holding exclusive access to the
  current tree. No child binding survives the layout call, so destroy/rebuild/surface replacement
  cannot reuse an old lease. A future retained desired-size binding must add surface-session identity,
  node incarnation, and explicit revocation without copying those runtime identities into the artifact.
- **Complexity gate:** binding, line/run projection, and child arrangement are monotonic
  `O(affected tree nodes + rich runs + graphemes + direct children)`. A process-global widget registry,
  per-child full-run scan, renderer markup parse, and cloned child draw list are prohibited.
- **Static evidence:** exact frame/render/hit, duplicate, missing, omitted, and renderer
  no-placeholder source regressions were added; the typed-slot source contract is included in the
complete 47/47 Runtime Text infrastructure batch (1.744 s); rustfmt, targeted whitespace, and
  `git diff --check` pass. This is not managed Cargo, runtime profile, power, or framebuffer evidence.
- Status: `dynamic_inline_widget_architecture_review_complete /
  fixed_size_direct_child_inline_widget_implemented / renderer_widget_placeholder_removed /
  typed_owner_local_widget_slot_implemented / current_tree_binding_nonretained /
  incremental_arrangement_root_bounded / static_checks_complete / managed_validation_pending`.
  Desired-size, retained session/incarnation lease, full G27/G28, managed Cargo,
  profile/RSS/power, WGPU/PNG, commit, WeCom, and `RTS-P1-032` remain open.

  admitted variable-size source, line, run, advance, editable, and composition data without any byte
  receipt. Entry count alone cannot demonstrate a bounded memory footprint or justify a byte cap.
- **Pre-optimization correction:** both lowest cache owners now maintain current and frame-peak
  estimated bytes across insert, same-key update, LRU eviction, trim, and clear. UI admission attributes
  source/style key heap to both caches and serializable resolved-layout DTO heap to the layout cache.
  Four aggregate profiling counters expose measure/layout current/peak bytes without source text or
  high-cardinality labels. Cache behavior and entry caps are unchanged.
- **Accounting boundary:** the receipt is explicitly a source/DTO-owned heap lower bound. It excludes
  opaque/shared glyph artifacts, allocator RSS, hash-table capacity, and allocator overhead. Shared
  artifacts cannot be charged per entry until a unique residency owner publishes a non-duplicating
  release receipt; `Arc::strong_count` is not a stable budget mechanism.
- **Static evidence:** focused regressions cover insert/update/evict/clear/peak accounting; rustfmt,
  targeted whitespace, owner/source guards, file-budget checks, and scoped diff-check pass. No managed
  Cargo, scale profile, allocation/RSS, power, Unreal timing, WGPU, or PNG evidence is claimed.
- Status: `cache_dto_source_residency_receipt_implemented /
  cache_update_evict_clear_accounting_implemented / static_checks_complete /

  duplicates, and invalid requests do not count as backend work. Session and UI publish aggregate
  low-cardinality counters without source text.

  synchronously. No Deferred result, partial run, scheduler, cancellation, deadline, CPU/memory cap,

- **Finding:** `FallbackResolution` survived itemization but the candidate work that produced it was

  Local Unreal retains composite selection and loading-face state through the shaping request. The

  probes, primary/candidate rejection, complete/partial visits, selection class, shaping attempts, and
  generation restarts. A transient completion envelope carries it beside the glyph run; session and
  parallel owners merge it, and generation retry accumulates discarded attempts.
- **Performance boundary:** counters are updated inside existing loops; candidate compiler probes are
  included and no second coverage pass/allocation is added. `ShapedGlyphRun`, shaped-cache key, serde,
  and resident-byte accounting remain untouched. Cache hits do not replay historical candidate work.
- **Evidence:** fixed-size, cache-hit zero-history-probe, retry accumulation, session merge, and profile
  regressions are present. Static rustfmt/diff/artifact-pollution checks pass. Managed Cargo,

  Pending, collection generation, policy/budget, backend capability, and tofu remain Runtime Font

  transient_completion_envelope_implemented / shaped_artifact_pollution_zero /
  full_font_resolve_outcome_open / static_checks_complete / managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-27 Runtime Text fallback cache structural profiling finding

- **Finding:** family, composite, candidate, resolution, and line-metric bounded caches share one
  mutable `Mutex<FallbackCacheState>`. A hit updates both the entry and indexed LRU, so it requires an
  exclusive lock; a miss may acquire the same lock repeatedly across cache layers. Cold composite
  compilation also remains inside that lock. Separately, a late whole-text primary rejection can make
  the accepted prefix participate in cluster coverage work again.
- **Reference:** local Unreal precompiles composite typefaces and sorted priority/ordinary codepoint
  ranges in `FCachedCompositeFontData`, binary-searches range selection, and has `FSlateTextShaper`
  linearly merge adjacent equal-face grapheme sections. This is evidence for an immutable compiled
  collection plus request itemizer boundary, not permission to weaken Zircon's full-cluster coverage.
- **Rejected measurement:** subtracting process-global cache counters around each shape request would
  overlap under parallel prewarm and double-attribute other workers. Emitting a profiler event at every
  lock would also contaminate the hot path being measured.
- **Correction:** all cache-state access now uses one measured boundary. Only test/profiling builds call
  `Instant`; global cache reports retain cumulative acquire/wait/hold values, while cache-owner TLS
  records the exact active itemization and publishes three fixed counters once on completion. Normal
  builds add no timing call and profile labels contain no face, family, codepoint, or request identity.
- **Gate:** 31-sample cold/warm profiling must independently test warm-hit serialization, cold compile
  convoy, late-primary duplicate probes, and resolution/candidate layer duplication. No sharding,
  snapshot cache, request memo, pre-scan removal, or resolver rewrite is authorized yet. Static
  formatting/diff checks pass; Cargo, CPU traces, timing/RSS/power, WGPU, and PNG remain pending.
  Status: `request_local_cache_lock_profile_implemented / structural_bottleneck_hypotheses_documented /
  resolver_algorithm_unchanged / structural_optimization_profile_gated / managed_profile_pending`.

  retained_paragraph_artifact_open / algorithm_unchanged / managed_profile_pending`.

  gives each model shaping/wrapping/direction/view dirty flags, and advances a model change counter.

  required `placement_frame` owns the paragraph or rich-cell slot used for physical-line selection
  and clipping admission. Plain/rich horizontal and `VerticalRl` producers publish both meanings;

  formerly reduced every UI dispatch result to a handled boolean. Editable Change/Submit and focus-loss
  Commit now retain their exact authored route/action identity; the compiled hot path uses the

  plaintext carrier. Rust 2024 formatting and static diff checks pass; managed Cargo has not run.

  qualified trusted session, one-shot consume contract, WOC adapter, retained state/history/log/
  crash/plugin/export, zeroization and secure IME remain open. Status:

## 2026-08-27 Runtime Text clipboard transaction finding

- **Finding:** keyboard cut deleted the selection before publishing a `WriteText` request that had no
  completion identity or result route. A missing/rejected host consumer therefore lost text; paste
  emitted an uncorrelated read request and could never apply a returned value safely.
- **Correction:** Runtime Interface now carries UUID transfer id, Copy/Cut/Paste intent,
  expected edit revision and typed read/write/failure outcome. Surface keeps at most one pending
  transfer per editable owner without a full text snapshot. Cut deletes only on a matching successful
  write result; paste uses the shared constraint/edit owner. Edit/focus/policy change, duplicate,
  wrong owner/outcome, clone/serde and detached owner fail closed. Secure paste result uses the same
  opaque event/redaction path; secure copy/cut remain denied.
- **Bridge correction:** each Surface manager now owns a 256-row bounded clipboard host queue and
  replaces an undrained request only for the same owner. The versioned dynamic DTO carries viewport,
  exact Surface index, UUID transfer and typed outcome. App completes the operation on the winit event
  thread and immediately returns the result to that exact Surface. Windows uses the live target HWND,
  `CF_UNICODETEXT` and movable global memory; non-Windows returns typed `Unsupported`. A NULL HWND was
  rejected after reviewing Unreal's `OpenClipboard(GetActiveWindow())` path because it can invalidate
  ownership after `EmptyClipboard` and make `SetClipboardData` fail.
- **Cost/evidence/open:** without pending transfer the mutation hook exits after an empty-store check;
  the first related change advances revision once and pending memory is `O(editable owners)`. Host
  collection is `O(S + R)` over declared surfaces and bounded queued rows; platform copy/read is
  `O(B)`. A 32 KiB UTF-8 cap keeps worst-case JSON control expansion below the 256 KiB host/event
  envelope. Rust 2024 formatting, scoped whitespace checks, queue contracts and source ownership
  guards are present; managed Cargo/system clipboard have not run. Window/seat/principal/deadline,
  timeout/fault injection, macOS/Linux/Web backend qualification and product WGPU acceptance remain
  open. Status: `runtime_transfer_contract_implemented_unvalidated /
  cut_data_loss_path_closed_unvalidated / surface_targeted_dynamic_abi_implemented_unvalidated /
  windows_cf_unicodetext_backend_implemented_unvalidated /
  bounded_queue_and_payload_contract_implemented_unvalidated / managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-27 Runtime editable-text property transaction finding

- **Finding:** one semantic text edit performed up to ten independent generic property mutations.
  A reserved or malformed dynamic value property could reject the value write while caret,
  selection and composition writes still committed. Business names such as `content` were also
  classified render-only, so a real value change could miss text/layout invalidation.
- **Correction:** `editable_text/property_transaction.rs` now prepares node/metadata, value-property
  domain and grapheme-valid state before any write, then commits a fixed ten-property batch. The
  batch helper no longer pre-marks node dirty; surface owns one style/component/binding projection,
  one dirty registration and one clipboard revision invalidation. Semantic value changes force
  layout/text/render dirty independent of the property name; focus-loss composition uses the same
  boundary. Accessibility SetValue/ReplaceSelectedText/SetTextSelection now prepares complete state
  from the same retained owner and commits through this boundary with `AccessibilityAction` source
  preserved in retained and component-state updates. Its old sequential helper, including the path
  that forgot to clear `composition_clauses`, was deleted.
- **Generic-entry follow-up:** editable body mutations entering through `UiSurface::mutate_property`
  now prepare the complete retained edit state and call the same transaction. Following Unreal's
  `SetEditableText` state repair, a changed display value preserves a valid caret or clamps it to a
  grapheme boundary, clears selection/composition, and publishes once; identical values remain
  no-op. Direct generic writes to derived caret/selection/composition fields are rejected. Stored
  `UiValue` and display text are separate, so external `NumberField` float updates remain floats.
  The reflected source-kind mapping is shared rather than duplicated by this route.
- **Cost/evidence/open:** candidates are a stack array and only changed fields allocate report
  entries. No full-tree snapshot or second document cache was added. Accessibility actions now emit
  one binding report rather than 8–9 per-field reports and register dirty once. Regressions cover
  zero partial state on reserved value property in widget and accessibility routes, clauses clearing,
  one revision advance for value edits and none for caret/selection-only edits. Rust 2024 formatting
  and scoped static diff checks pass. A focused Windows no-run Cargo attempt produced no compiler
  output before its 184-second timeout and was terminated; it is not a pass. Managed Cargo, fault injection,
  CPU/allocation/RSS/power and WGPU have not run. Product document identity/revision, origin/range
  receipt, component reducer convergence, focused bound-text conflict policy, numeric internal
  parse/commit, history/rebase and
  platform composition ack remain open. Status:
  `surface_property_prepare_commit_implemented_unvalidated /
  widget_and_accessibility_projection_converged_unvalidated /
  generic_external_projection_converged_unvalidated /
  derived_property_write_bypass_closed_unvalidated /
  partial_metadata_write_path_closed / composition_clause_clear_path_closed /
  semantic_text_dirty_invalidation_implemented /
  product_document_transaction_open / managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-27 Runtime editable-text component authority finding

- **Finding:** Material editable descriptors exposed raw `KeyboardText` while Surface already owned
  keyboard/text/IME/clipboard/accessibility editing. `state_reducer/text_input.rs` independently
  reconstructed text/caret/selection/composition, applied Insert and wrote every field back. This was
  a public second editing authority; SearchField/FieldEditor/SourceEditor classification and primary
  value-property choice also differed between Surface, renderer and validation.
- **Correction:** editable Material descriptors now expose only semantic `Focus`, `ValueChanged` and
  `Commit`. The component edit-state/Insert/write-back implementation was deleted; the remaining
  reducer mirrors committed semantic values and owns validation only. Raw `KeyboardText` remains for
  menu typeahead and command palette. Text-input role/component aliases live in one
  `UiWidgetBehavior` classifier. V1 compilation infers one value property using
  `query -> value -> value_text -> text`, preserves authored overrides, and Surface fallback recognizes
  `value_text`, so FieldEditor/SourceEditor route through the same transaction with their schema key.
- **Structure/cost:** about 315 lines of duplicate reducer authority were removed. Value-property
  inference runs at descriptor compilation/retained-state resolution and adds no per-glyph cache or
  grapheme-loop scan. New component tests require raw events to fail without mutation and semantic
  events/state properties to remain declared; Surface regressions cover FieldEditor/SourceEditor
  transaction publication.
- **Evidence/open:** Rust 2024 formatting, scoped `git diff --check`, raw-event descriptor scan and
  production reducer-reference scan pass. A Windows interface test attempt remained in dependency
  compilation at 64 seconds and its owned Cargo/Rustc children were terminated; there is no dynamic
  test pass. A subsequent `cargo check -p zircon_runtime_interface --lib` reused the E-drive target
  and passed in 114.5 seconds with nine existing warnings, so the interface production lib compiles;
  runtime and tests remain unvalidated. Status: `component_raw_keyboard_edit_authority_removed_unvalidated /
  canonical_editable_value_property_inference_implemented_unvalidated /
  semantic_component_projection_only_unvalidated / interface_lib_compile_passed /
  managed_validation_pending`. Product document
  authority, focused binding/rebase, numeric internal parse/commit, profile/power and WGPU evidence
  remain open.

## 2026-08-27 Runtime focused bound-text policy finding

- **Reference finding:** Unreal `SlateEditableTextLayout.cpp:3622-3636` performs only password and
  marshaller refresh while an editable is focused; it does not replace the active editable text from
  the bound attribute. `OnBoundTextChanged:4508-4547` skips focused bound refresh unless an explicit
  SetText/LoadText-style force review is active, and only broadcasts when the value actually differs.
- **Zircon defect:** `UiPropertyMutationRequest` defaults to `RuntimeState`; its optional
  `binding_source_kind` classifies downstream binding publication, not whether the request is a bound
  model refresh or an explicit replacement. Surface metadata is currently both the model-visible
  property and the editable buffer. `mutate_editable_text_property` therefore applies every body
  update immediately, clears selection/composition and can overwrite focused user work. A focus
  early-return would instead silently lose the model update.
- **Required architecture:** the product document/edit session must retain separate model and editable
  identities, a typed refresh origin and expected revision. At most one latest pending model refresh
  may be retained per owner while focused. Explicit replacement uses a force-review transaction;
  blur/commit either applies a still-compatible pending revision or emits a typed conflict/rebase
  receipt. Secure pending values belong to the secure store and must be invalidated on detach,
  policy change and session teardown. Generic `Accepted/Unchanged/Rejected` cannot falsely report a
  deferred bound refresh as committed.
- **Correction:** a versioned `UiTextModelUpdateRequest/Receipt` now distinguishes bound refresh from
  explicit Set/Load and qualifies every request with the expected document UUID/revision. The paired
  `UiInputManager` retains at most one latest pending refresh per owner while focused. Blur applies it
  only when the committed key still matches; otherwise it publishes a content-free stale conflict.
  Explicit Set/Load applies immediately and uses the committed preedit base, so transient IME text is
  neither accepted as the document source nor counted in the replacement range. Secure pending text
  resides only in the Surface secure store; manager rows retain metadata and byte counts. An opaque
  clear-only handle lets Surface switch and manager Drop revoke the prior store without giving the
  manager a plaintext read path. Follow-up lifetime review found that ordinary `String` release did
  not erase superseded/rejected pending values. The store now retains `Zeroizing<String>` and uses
  `mem::take` only on accepted transfer, so overwrite/discard/clear/Drop erase pending allocations
  without adding a second full-value clone.
- **Boundary/status:** the queue is capped at 256 pending+terminal rows, 4 MiB per value and 16 MiB
  aggregate. The current `BTreeMap` path is `O(log E)` for `E <= 256`, while accepted Surface
  replacement remains `O(N)` because the property model still owns full strings. Generic editable
  mutation remains an explicit replacement compatibility path and is not relabeled as bound refresh.
  This closes only the pending-store allocation lifetime: request rejection, component state,
  document/history, layout/platform intermediates and crash dumps still contain ordinary strings, so
  the engine-wide zeroization finding remains open.
  Source regressions cover defer/apply/conflict/supersession/secure/oversize/explicit/preedit behavior;
  managed Runtime, product profile/power, platform IME and WGPU are not yet validated. Status:
  `unreal_focused_refresh_policy_reviewed /
  focused_bound_model_update_gateway_implemented_unvalidated /
  revision_compare_and_swap_conflict_implemented_unvalidated /
  secure_pending_surface_owned_unvalidated /
  secure_pending_drop_zeroization_implemented_unvalidated /
  persistent_secure_document_zeroization_open / managed_acceptance_pending`.

## 2026-08-27 Runtime NumberField value-kind finding

- **Finding:** Material `NumberField.value` is schema-typed `Float`, but Surface classified the widget
  as TextInput and `commit_editable_text_properties` always constructed `UiValue::String` from the
  edit buffer. The transaction then wrote the String into retained metadata and component projection
  without a schema/type preflight. Existing numeric tests covered arrows, drag and direct Float commit,
  not character input, IME, intermediate-invalid text, blur or cancel.
- **Unreal reference:** `SSpinBox.cpp:937-990` keeps `EditableText` separate from `ValueAttribute`,
  filters characters, optionally publishes only fully numeric per-key strings, and parses again on
  commit through `INumericTypeInterface`. `CommitValue:993-1076` clamps the typed result, publishes
  typed changed/committed delegates and updates a formatted value cache. `SNumericEntryBox.h:719-741`
  has the same parse-before-typed-event boundary.
- **Correction:** editable property transaction preflight now compares the retained and proposed
  `UiValue` variants. A String edit targeting a retained Float returns `value_kind_mismatch` before
  any value/caret/selection/component/binding/dirty write. External Float-to-Float mutation remains
  supported. The regression uses actual `NumberField`, canonical `value = Float(42.0)` and text input,
  and requires Float/caret/event/binding to remain unchanged.
- **Open/performance:** this is a constant-time type guard and correctness hardening, not a numeric
  parser or measured optimization. A real NumberField still needs a separate bounded edit buffer,
  locale/type interface, intermediate-invalid state, parse receipt, typed change/commit policy,
  clamp/format cache, escape/blur behavior and focused external refresh through the product edit
  session. No CPU/allocation/RSS/power profile or dynamic test pass is claimed. Status:
  `numeric_value_kind_corruption_fail_closed_unvalidated / number_edit_buffer_open /
  locale_parse_commit_open / managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-27 Runtime Autocomplete editable-render property finding

- **Finding:** Surface already treated Autocomplete as editable and selected `query` over the selected
  `value`; its regression edits query without overwriting value. After render classification was
  converged on the shared editable classifier, `resolve_editable_text_value` still independently
  defaulted to `value`. Raw/V2 metadata could therefore edit query while visible layout, caret,
  selection and composition geometry were built against the selected value.
- **Correction/cost:** a metadata-level canonical property resolver now owns explicit override then
  `query -> value -> value_text -> text`. Input obtains its owned property name once at transaction
  entry; render borrows `&str` and uses the same key for visible and editable state, so there is no
  new per-frame property-name allocation. A render resolver regression keeps `query = needle` and
  `value = asset://selected` distinct and requires visible/editable text plus caret to use query.
- **Evidence/open:** Rustfmt and scoped diff/reference scans pass. The focused NumberField runtime test
  remained silent in compilation for about 70 seconds; its exact Cargo/Rustc tree was verified and
  terminated, so no runtime test pass is claimed. Status:
  `autocomplete_query_render_edit_property_converged_unvalidated /
  canonical_metadata_property_resolver_shared / managed_validation_pending`.
