## 2026-08-27 Runtime public text edit snapshot finding

- **Finding:** public `UiTextEdit` serialized a raw `UiTextEditAction` plus complete before/after
  `UiEditableTextState` snapshots inside `UiWidgetEvent::TextEditChange`. It had no stable document
  identity, no revision fence or schema version, exposed composition/edit payload through a broad
  public contract, and made event size `O(document length)`. No runtime/app/editor producer consumed
  that contract, so the snapshots were neither a product session nor useful incremental evidence.
- **Correction:** the old DTO and event field were hard removed. `TextEditChange` now carries only a
  versioned `UiTextEditReceipt` containing non-nil document UUID, consecutive previous/current
  revisions, typed source/kind, old/new byte ranges and final byte selection with focus affinity.
  Validation rejects unsupported schema, nil identity, revision jump/exhaustion and reversed ranges;
  deserialization invokes the same validation, including when nested in `UiWidgetEvent`.
  `UiTextEditAction` remains an in-process input intent and no longer crosses the public boundary.
- **Producer preflight:** the non-Clone internal document now signs its public UUID and places it,
  previous/current byte lengths and dirty ranges in the changed receipt. Public projection no longer
  accepts a caller-selected document id or document length. It performs no source read/copy and
  rejects owner change, non-consecutive revision, inconsistent range/document delta, u32 narrowing,
  old/new bounds and selection bounds before constructing the public receipt.
- **Boundary/performance:** receipt publication is fixed-size and content-free; it does not hash or
  copy source text and cannot leak a secure document snapshot. The internal `TextDocument` still has
  no product registry/producer mapping, product snapshot-lease consumer, history/rebase or
  range/source validation.
  Do not embed it in clone/serde `UiSurface` or claim M1 closed. Rustfmt, source scan and diff check are
  static evidence only; the interface change has not received a current Cargo pass. Status:
  `public_text_edit_snapshot_event_removed_unvalidated /
  versioned_document_edit_receipt_contract_implemented_unvalidated /
  internal_document_receipt_projection_implemented_unvalidated /
  runtime_document_service_gateway_open / product_snapshot_lease_integration_open /
  m1_document_authority_open`.

## 2026-08-27 Runtime revision-bound snapshot lease finding

- **Finding:** crate-private piece storage exposed only `snapshot() -> String`; every consumer could
  flatten the complete source again without proving its revision. The grapheme source index did
  exactly that and then scanned the temporary copy. Future layout/IME/a11y/host consumers would make
  repeated `O(N)` copies for one revision. Derived `Debug` also printed original/addition content,
  which is not an acceptable secure-document logging boundary.
- **Correction:** each document revision now owns one lazy contiguous `Arc<str>` snapshot slot.
  Initial source reuses the original Arc. Repeated leases share it; a changed edit creates a new lazy
  slot after fallible prepare succeeds, old leases stay stable, and typed no-op retains pointer
  identity. Source-index rebuild borrows the lease directly and no longer creates a second N-byte
  String. Document/lease Debug publishes only identity/revision/length/block counts.
- **Cost/open:** an explicitly requested changed revision still has one `O(N)` flatten and one
  retained N-byte allocation; grapheme boundary construction is still an `O(N)` full scan. Product
  registry/public UUID mapping, Surface consumer, snapshot byte/age/count budget, old-revision
  retention, zeroization and managed allocation/RSS/power evidence remain open. Status:
  `revision_bound_snapshot_lease_implemented_unvalidated /
  single_flatten_per_requested_revision_implemented_unvalidated /
  source_index_secondary_source_copy_removed_unvalidated /
  document_debug_source_redacted / product_registry_open /
  snapshot_budget_and_managed_profile_pending`.

## 2026-08-28 Runtime retained document storage growth correction

- **Measured finding:** the pre-change 17-scenario, 31-sample matrix confirmed two structural defects.
  One immutable addition chunk per replacement made sequential typing rebuild a growing piece list;
  10k tail inserts had a 1.711 second p50 and requested 8.127 GB. Independently,
  separator-neutral preparation copied/reparsed a complete hard line; one tail insert into a
  one-million-character line had a 6.700 millisecond p50 and about 2 MB of counted allocation.
- **Correction:** `TextDocument` now owns one append-only addition `String`, and pieces reference
  logical ranges in that source. Sequential additions coalesce. A prepared separator-neutral edit
  wholly inside one hard-line content range updates only that stable model's content length; CR/LF
  insert/remove/split/join, including CRLF-interior insertion, remains on the reparse path.
  `text/document/report.rs` publishes source count/bytes/capacity and does not double-count content.
- **Post evidence:** the identical matrix records 10k tail inserts at 4.508 milliseconds p50 and
  3.643 MB counted allocation (379.46x and 2,231.08x improvements). One-million-character 100-edit
  tail/middle lanes are 0.061/0.034 milliseconds p50 and retain two/three pieces. 49 production
  document/hard-line/store tests plus three structural harness guards pass. Baseline, post, and
  comparison JSONL/CSV evidence is under `docs/tests/runtime/text` with hashes in the Runtime82
  optimization report.
- **Boundary/reference:** Unreal's target-line mutation and distinct split/join operations support
  this retained-line/local-dirty boundary, not a copied container. WPR CPU stack and power capture is
  blocked by Windows policy `0xc5585011`; sampled stacks, energy, matched Unreal runtime, product
  Surface authority, full Runtime validation and WGPU/PNG remain open. Status:
  `append_only_addition_source_implemented / local_hard_line_edit_implemented /
  baseline_and_post_matrix_complete / structural_hotspots_eliminated /
  power_and_matched_unreal_pending`.

## 2026-08-27 Runtime document service lifecycle and admission finding

- **Finding:** registering the retained document as a UI module-global manager would mix otherwise
  surface-local node/edit lifetimes. Embedding it in `UiSurface` would be worse: that type is Clone and
  serde, while a mutable document authority must not branch identity/revision. Current product code
  already pairs each runtime surface with one `UiInputManager`.
- **Correction:** document replace now uses prepare/commit. Prepare performs every fallible structural
  calculation without mutation; commit rechecks the expected key. A crate-private session store is
  constructed only with explicit count/byte/addition-source/piece/snapshot/lease limits, performs admission
  before commit/flatten, returns typed content-free errors, and decrements active lease count/bytes on
  `Drop`. It has no `Default` and is not globally registered.
- **Follow-up correction:** each product `RuntimeUiSurface` now retains the document session in its
  paired `UiInputManager`. Exact edit intent passes document admission and Surface preflight before a
  thin dual commit; only then does the manager publish the content-free versioned receipt. Surface
  instance identity and topology-gated owner synchronization reclaim detached bindings without a
  per-event full scan. Delta undo/redo reuses the same gateway; focus loss is state-only cancellation
  and clears only the exact owner's history.
- **Open:** numeric product thresholds, incremental grapheme handles, automatic three-way merge after
  a stale model conflict, and managed product validation remain open. The focused model refresh
  gateway now performs bounded defer plus expected-revision CAS and emits conflict instead of
  overwriting user edits. Aggregate store reporting was measured
  as the multi-document bottleneck and is now constant-time; the current `BTreeMap` lookup remains
  `O(log D)` because profiling did not justify another index. The direct-source document suite passes
  54/54 and the focused history harness passes 3/3, while product Runtime tests remain unvalidated.
  Status:
  `document_prepare_commit_boundary_implemented /
  explicit_limit_session_store_implemented /
  global_manager_registration_rejected / surface_input_session_integration_implemented_unvalidated /
  content_free_receipt_and_delta_history_implemented_unvalidated /
  focus_loss_state_only_owner_implemented_unvalidated /
  focused_model_refresh_gateway_implemented_unvalidated /
  product_thresholds_three_way_merge_and_managed_profile_pending`.

## 2026-08-28 ECS archetype constant-equality finding

- **Finding:** `ArchetypeIndex::PartialEq` ignored its peer and always returned `true`. Because
  `World` derives `PartialEq`, worlds with different archetype signatures or different entity rows
  could compare equal, masking storage-topology regressions and making equality-based receipts
  unreliable. This is Runtime60 `RECS-P1-10`, not a micro-optimization finding.
- **Correction:** an owner-local borrowed `ArchetypeTopologySnapshot` now compares both canonical
  lookup indexes and each ordered record's ID, signature and entity rows. `ArchetypeIndex::eq`
  delegates to that receipt. Performance counters and membership revision history are deliberately
  excluded; no public API, duplicate registry, persisted snapshot or compatibility path was added.
- **Scope/evidence:** three Rust regressions cover signature divergence, row divergence and
  diagnostics/history exclusion. The focused guard locks the implementation and five status
  mirrors. Unreal Mass handle identity/version separation is the primary reference and Bevy's
  world-local ID/generation/container separation is the cross-check. Equality now has explicit
  linear structural cost only when requested; no frame/query hot-path or performance claim was
  introduced. Cargo/product/profile validation remains deferred. Status:
  `runtime_08_15_archetype_topology_equality_receipt_static_passed_cargo_deferred`.

## 2026-08-29 Runtime NumberField focused Float authority finding

- **Finding:** the String document model-update gateway correctly rejected `NumberField`, while the
  generic Float property mutation intentionally acted as an explicit replacement and closed the edit
  buffer. Consequently, a bound Float refresh had no product route that could update canonical value
  without destroying focused user input. Reusing String document revisions would also make the wrong
  authority responsible for numeric CAS.
- **Correction:** `NumberField` now owns canonical and edit-base revisions inside the shared numeric
  property transaction. A separate versioned Float request/receipt and manager-owned numeric model
  identity implement synchronous `BoundRefresh` and `ExplicitSetValue`: bound refresh advances only
  canonical revision while preserving an active buffer/base; stale Enter reports conflict, stale blur
  adopts latest canonical display, and explicit replacement closes editing. Revision exhaustion,
  malformed state, stale CAS, and non-finite values reject before the metadata batch. Model-key
  issuance also requires a finite canonical TOML Float. The tree now assigns one monotonic insertion
  serial at `UiTreeNodes::insert`, retains its high-water mark across `clear()`, and exposes that value
  as read-only node incarnation. Numeric authority is stamped with only the owning node incarnation:
  retained node-pool detach/reinsert reissues the UUID for the same `UiNodeId`, while unrelated sibling
  insert/remove, layout, and property changes preserve it. Topology generation only gates one bounded
  detached-owner prune; stable input does not scan owners or maintain a second incarnation table.
- **Structure/evidence:** the gateway adds no second pending queue or String document/cache owner. Five
  fixed counters cover request, focused preservation, conflict, revision advance, and rejection.
  Product-source regressions cover focus/conflict/explicit/stale/non-finite/revision-exhaustion,
  generic internal-state bypass rejection, generic/accessibility writer revision advance, and
  session-identity replacement. Retained same-node-id replacement now proves UUID invalidation, stale
  key conflict, and zero write; malformed non-Float canonical state proves fail-closed key issuance.
  Unrelated sibling insertion and detachment also prove byte-for-byte model-key stability across both
  topology generations.
  touched production owners remain below 800 lines and scoped Rustfmt passes. Managed Cargo,
  platform/WGPU/PNG, matched Unreal runtime, profile, and power evidence remain pending. Status:
  `runtime_text_08_number_field_focused_float_refresh_static_implemented_managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-29 Runtime packaged default CompositeFont ownership finding

- **Finding:** the packaged default manifest and two-face TTC were already embedded and registered from
  cooked bytes, but bootstrap only retained the faces and a private Latin alias. It did not activate the
  manifest `CompositeFont` or establish the packaged UI family. The only active composite/default-family
  state belonged to the project projection, so removing or lacking `res://fonts/default.font.toml`
  discarded the CJK route and fell through to host `fontdb` generic defaults.
- **Correction:** `FontDatabase` now owns independent runtime-baseline and project-override composite
  descriptors/indexes plus runtime/project UI families. Fallback precedence is explicit request, project,
  then runtime baseline; clearing a project family restores the runtime family. One cache rebuild owner
  recompiles both composite indexes after policy or face changes, and render-input equivalence includes
  both layers. Bootstrap installs the embedded manifest composite and `Fira Mono` only after registering
  its two cooked TTC faces. The database also retains that baseline's primary face: SDF resolution still
  prefers an attached project owner, but otherwise consumes the embedded face without manifest/source I/O.
- **Evidence/status:** focused source regressions cover pre-project zh-Hans selection of TTC face 1,
  project override/clear restoration, effective UI-family restoration and renderer default-asset removal.
  Rust 2024 formatting, scoped diff checks and direct-call scans pass. No Cargo, clean-package, WGPU/PNG,
  profile/RSS/power or matched Unreal runtime was executed. Status:
  `runtime_text_01_packaged_default_composite_baseline_static_implemented_managed_product_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-29 Runtime clean-process default face admission finding

- **Finding:** `TextFontRequest::default()` intentionally carries no family. `font_query_for_text_style`
  therefore produced an empty family, while `FontDatabase::match_face` searched only explicit and public
  platform/asset fallback families. The embedded Fira Mono primary and CJK sub-font were neither, so a
  clean process with system fonts disabled could return `FontUnavailable` before CompositeFont fallback.
  Existing service tests used the process-global shared database and could be masked by earlier mutations.
- **Correction:** primary matching now follows explicit family, project default family, runtime default
  primary, runtime default family, then platform/asset fallback. Runtime/project default mutations detach
  matching and fallback caches, so a cached empty query cannot retain the previous project face. The
  packaged primary remains an exact private face rather than being injected into the public fallback list.
- **Evidence/status:** local database regressions lock runtime/project/clear/explicit precedence and cache
  invalidation. A fresh embedded database shapes `A界`, projects neutral handles, and requires both Latin
  and zh-Hans rasterizable glyphs to resolve to live packaged faces. Rustfmt and static boundaries pass;
  Cargo, WGPU/PNG, profile/RSS/power and product package execution remain pending. Status:
  `runtime_text_01_clean_process_default_face_admission_static_implemented_managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-29 Runtime FontObject owner-scope finding

- **Finding:** UI preparation registered a `FontAsset` under its URI, but canonical shaping built a family
  query from `font_family.or(font)`. An asset-only request therefore queried `res://...font.toml` as a family
  and could silently fall through to the project/runtime default. The asset's own CompositeFont was not
  retained by the database resolver. Shaped-cache keys also collapsed asset and family into one optional
  string, while fixed-height line-metric certification used only the global font chain. Owner attachment was
  excluded from render-input equivalence, so making a second logical FontObject selectable did not advance
  generation. The combined behavior could render and lay out with different faces or reuse a stale run.
- **Correction:** `style.font` is now the exact FontObject owner and `font_family` its optional owner-local
  typeface. Database owner state retains ordered sources, fallback declarations and CompositeFont; generation
  publication builds an `owner -> Arc<CompositeFontIndex>` table. Primary, cluster fallback, line-metric
  envelope and primary-only coverage certificates consume the same owner scope. Shaped/fallback cache keys
  retain owner independently, and owner attach/remove now advances generation while shared physical faces
  remain deduplicated. Owner-local same-family faces exclude global homonyms; owner fallback adds only the
  database base/platform chain, not other loaded assets' fallback declarations.
- **Evidence/status:** source regressions cover asset-before-project-default primary selection, CJK selection
  from the requested asset CompositeFont, cross-owner fallback isolation, distinct owners sharing a typeface
  name, generation advancement for shared faces, and line-metric envelope selection. Asset composite compile
  count is fixed at generation publication and does not increase during the request. Rust 2024 formatting,
  scoped diff checks and retired URI-as-family scans pass. Cargo, real multilingual shape/raster, WGPU/PNG,
  cache/profile/RSS/power and matched Unreal execution were not run. Status:
  `runtime_text_font_object_owner_scope_static_implemented_managed_product_validation_pending`.
- **Final SDF consumer follow-up:** valid shaped face/instance handles already matched Unreal's
  `FShapedGlyphEntry(FontFaceData, GlyphIndex)` atlas boundary, but scalar/stale-handle recovery still called the
  legacy global resolver with `composite=None`. It now reuses the request-owner resolver and discards stale glyph
  IDs before scalar lookup. A two-face FontAsset regression locks CJK recovery to the owner's composite member;
  focused Cargo and real raster evidence remain pending.
- **Unavailable-owner follow-up:** an explicit missing FontObject previously left its local typeface in the global
  query, so an unrelated same-name face could impersonate the asset in shaping and metric certification. A shared
  borrowed-or-owned query constraint now clears only that family list before default recovery; registered owners
  stay allocation-free. Shaping and real-face metric regressions are implemented; dynamic gates remain pending.
- **Registered-owner provenance follow-up:** candidate deduplication also erased whether a family came from the
  requested local typeface or an authored external fallback. The resolver now carries `OwnerLocalOnly` versus
  `OwnerThenGlobal` through one O(n) normalized dedupe pass. Missing local typefaces cannot hit global homonyms;
  explicit external fallback remains covered. Cargo/profile/product evidence is still pending.
- **Owner-face materialization follow-up:** the first scoped implementation reconstructed all owner faces from
  source keys into a fresh vector for primary and every candidate-family query. Registration now publishes the
  ordered face set once as `Arc<[FontFaceId]>`; shaping, metric, and SDF recovery borrow it. Candidate ordering and
  coverage are unchanged. The allocation/timing effect remains unmeasured until the managed profile gate.

  `ResolvedGlyphStatus`, Native/SDF/WGPU pixels, profile/RSS/power, and product qualification remain open.

## 2026-08-29 Runtime Text generation snapshot follow-up

- Review found that canonical shaping sampled a process-global generation before work, while the cosmic

  the bytes/backend catalog corresponding to the sampled identity.
- Shared publication now stores `Arc<FontDatabase>` and returns `FontCollectionSnapshot { generation, Arc }`.
  Canonical shaping and cosmic fallback/direct work consume that exact snapshot; old snapshots remain readable
  after a new generation is published. Legacy mutable renderer clones are separately profiled.
- Source tests cover stable Arc reuse, retired generation retention, and forcing cosmic to use an old supplied
  snapshot after a newer global publication. Rustfmt, scoped diff-check, and no-global-reprobe guards pass.
- The follow-up ownership cut moves registry/snapshot/metrics under `FontCollectionService`; face handles now carry
  collection identity, and canonical/artifact projection plus SDF consumers use their supplied service. UI raster
  views retain matching database and registry snapshots, so a publication cannot reinterpret an in-flight glyph.
  Foreign-collection and post-publication lease regressions are present. `RFF-P1-015` is statically implemented but
  managed Cargo/WGPU acceptance is pending; session manager injection, backend face-slot reclamation, performance,
  RSS, and power remain open.
- Renderer follow-up found process-generation reads in plan caching, retained segment publication, and artifact

  constructor chain now accepts one explicit service; all three boundaries use the complete revision and reject a
  foreign collection at the same generation. Process task-pool budget selection remains reusable without selecting
  process font state. Static checks pass; Core manager/window/PIE injection and every dynamic/product gate remain
  open, so this does not close `RFF-P1-013/014/017`.

## 2026-08-29 Runtime Text Core service and first-layout admission follow-up

- **Finding:** collection ownership had reached screen-space renderer constructors, but dynamic Runtime UI
  surfaces still built and laid out before project font assets were admitted. Renderer-side font cache then
  registered the same assets later, allowing a first-frame layout/render mismatch; raw plan fallback also called
  the process-level `TextLayoutService` instead of the renderer-owned collection.
- **Correction:** `TextModule` now registers one Core Services `FontCollectionService`; Graphics and dynamic UI
  resolve that exact service. Runtime UI loads all retained surfaces, extracts default/explicit font asset
  dependencies, admits them once, then performs the first layout. Renderer plan stores raw batches and prepare
  fallback shaping calls the explicit collection only. `UiSurface::compute_layout` now invokes the existing
  font-generation invalidation hook at its layout boundary. Font registration/retire and default projection
  policy live in `text/font/runtime_asset.rs`; UI renderer font cache no longer owns a second registration
  algorithm.
- **Evidence/status:** module isolation, same-generation foreign-collection rejection, private-collection
  fallback-handle provenance, dependency deduplication, scoped rustfmt and diff checks are present. No Cargo,
  WGPU/PNG, window/PIE, profile/RSS/power or matched Unreal run was executed; screenshot evidence remains
  validation-pending and must be written only below `docs/tests/runtime/text`, never `target/`. Status:
  `runtime_text_core_service_first_layout_admission_static_implemented_managed_validation_pending`.
- **Historical lifecycle finding (superseded by the current-source correction below):** renderer font records
  previously cached admissions without a project/session-scoped release contract. A previous project's unused owner
  could remain in the collection and affect fallback before the next render prepare; dynamic UI also could not delete
  everything absent from its own surfaces because HUD, menu, and other consumers may share the collection.

- **2026-08-29 current-source correction:** the ownership portion is now implemented in Text Core. A non-Clone
  `RuntimeFontAssetClaimScope` records aggregate consumer counts in `FontCollectionService`; Runtime UI claims its
  complete dependency set before first layout, and the screen-space renderer reconciles dependencies before
  collection refresh. The last scope release retires all newly unclaimed asset owners in one database
  mutation/publication, while renderer-local ready/missing/error records are pruned for later re-admission. This
  closes the old renderer-cache-only lifetime design and prevents shared HUD/menu owners from being deleted by one
  consumer. Release plus changed/new admission is staged in one collection mutation/publication, so an A -> B
  dependency transition publishes one generation. The ownership and transaction design are statically implemented;
  project-switch/hot-reload residency, fallback isolation, Cargo, WGPU/PNG and profile/RSS/power gates remain
  required before acceptance. No product acceptance or commit is implied. Status:
  `runtime_text_font_owner_claim_scope_static_implemented /
  release_plus_admission_single_publication_static_implemented /
  managed_lifecycle_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-29 Light Grid Projection Correctness Finding

- **Finding:** the CPU light-grid builder used `ortho_size * 0.5` even though the shared camera
  convention treats `ortho_size` as half-height, and rejected any perspective sphere whose center
  produced `clip.w <= 0`, including camera-inside and near-plane-crossing lights.
- **Correction:** route projection construction through the shared `ViewProjectionMatrixPair` owner,
  use the canonical orthographic half-height, and for near-crossing spheres use a
  conservative near-plane projection and full-tile coverage when the camera is inside the sphere.
  Preserve the existing depth-range rejection for spheres wholly behind the camera.
- **Evidence/status:** RED isolation reproduced both failures; the five focused regressions and E-drive
  production-file rustc harness pass, with scoped formatting/diff checks green. This does not claim
  performance improvement or close the broader reversed-Z/oblique/jittered/stereo/viewport-subrect
  bounds-library work. Status:
  `runtime_render05_light_grid_projection_correctness_isolated_tests_passed_managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text partial-layout suppression finding

- The shared text layout pipeline already propagated `Deferred`/`Failed` outcomes through measure,
  line-break, boundary correction and UI paragraph owners, but three source-range guards still used
  `continue` after a failed UTF-8 projection. That could silently drop a grapheme or break cluster and
  publish a layout that looked successful but was incomplete.
- The guards now fail closed at the owning stage: hard-line source extraction returns `LayoutFailed`,
  malformed backend cluster ranges return `BidiInvariant`, and glyph-wrap/corrected-range projection
  returns `LayoutFailed`; Cosmic line/glyph projection now rejects invalid checked ranges before
  hard-line normalization. Only a legal zero-width virtual anchor is exempt. No lower stage fabricates
  geometry, and the parent owner retains the typed disposition for its existing fallback policy.

  dependency target writes failed before `zircon_runtime` source compilation. WGPU/PNG, profile, RSS,
  power or Unreal comparison was not run. Status: `runtime_text_partial_layout_suppression_removed /
  range_invariant_fail_closed_static_implemented / managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text backend source identity invariant follow-up

- **Finding:** `BackendShapeRequest` carried a local UTF-8 view and an absolute source range, but
  canonicalization did not require the range's byte span to cover that view. A malformed request
  could enter fallback, shaped-cache identity, or backend projection with incompatible source
  coordinates. The line-break path's local hard-line slice plus matching absolute offset was
  reviewed and confirmed correct; it was not rewritten.
- **Correction:** `BackendShapeRequest::canonicalized` now performs checked subtraction and requires
  `source_range.end - source_range.start == text.len()`. Reversed or mismatched requests fail closed
  as `TextLayoutError::BidiInvariant` before language normalization, fallback, cache lookup, or
  backend shaping. A legal non-zero absolute start remains supported for local slices.
- **Evidence/status:** model tests cover legal non-zero offset, mismatched span, and reversed range;
  targeted Rustfmt and scoped diff checks pass. Composite activation is 20/20, the text pointer
  contract is 1/1, segment cache is 13/13, decoration source-map pressure is 4/4, and layout-order
  authority is 4/4. The framework boundary script timed out in this environment. Cargo, WGPU/PNG,
  profiling, RSS/power, and matched Unreal validation remain pending. Status:
`runtime_text_backend_source_identity_guard_static_implemented /
malformed_source_range_rejected_before_backend / managed_validation_pending`.

managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text glyph artifact source-slice admission follow-up

- **Finding:** artifact builders checked that line/run ranges were numerically contained by the
  layout, but did not verify that those ranges were UTF-8 sliceable in the exact source snapshot.

  `artifact_line_source_ranges_are_sliceable` before shaping/projection. Every line and run range,
  including zero-width virtual anchors, must pass `str::get` against the source origin; malformed
  ranges fail closed as `LayoutFailed`. Valid generated ellipsis/replacement anchors remain

  a valid end-of-source anchor. Targeted Rustfmt and scoped diff checks pass. Cargo, WGPU/PNG,

`runtime_text_glyph_artifact_source_slice_admission_static_implemented /
  virtual_anchor_boundary_preserved / managed_validation_pending`.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text rich-source contract follow-up

- **Finding:** `RichAdvanceIndex::source_spans` previously skipped a missing or malformed rich run
  and let materialization publish partial/empty geometry. That violated the source owner contract
  and could silently lose styled text.
- **Correction:** rich index, rich artifact, and prewarm-cache consumers now reuse one
  `RichTextLayoutSource` validator for run lookup, non-sentinel strictly increasing parent source
  indices, non-overlap, checked numeric bounds, and UTF-8 sliceability. Legal uncovered source gaps remain base-style text; malformed layout returns
  `TextLayoutError::LayoutFailed` and malformed prewarm input is discarded.
- **Evidence/status:** source-contract regressions cover empty/partial valid input plus overlap,
  empty, and out-of-bounds ranges; targeted Rustfmt and scoped diff checks pass. Managed Cargo,
  WGPU/PNG, profiling, RSS/power, and Unreal-matched validation remain pending. Status:
  `runtime_text_rich_source_contract_fail_closed_static_implemented /
  base_style_gap_fill_preserved / managed_validation_pending`。

The same review also closed three rich line-range extraction fail-open branches: an invalid hard-line
UTF-8 slice in horizontal glyph-wrap, horizontal word-wrap, or VerticalRl word-wrap now returns
`LayoutFailed` instead of silently dropping the line. Normal range generation and fallback behavior
are unchanged; the static contract covers all three owners. Managed validation remains pending.

Rich UI item projection now applies the same rule to malformed internal `LayoutItem` run identity or
source range: it returns `LayoutFailed` rather than treating the item as a successful zero-advance
projection. This keeps incomplete geometry from crossing the rich layout boundary.

The VerticalRl rich projection now uses the same typed failure for invalid numeric or UTF-8 column
ranges; only the intentional non-Vertical/Plain route retains `Ready(None)` as its capability fallback.

Rich table layout also no longer clamps malformed absolute table/cell ranges with `min/max` or
`saturating_sub`. Checked projection admission preserves legal empty cells and rejects reversed,
cross-projection, parent-outside, and non-UTF-8 ranges before sizing or cell layout.

Table segment projection now rejects reversed, out-of-bounds, and non-UTF-8 local ranges before
calling `UiParsedText::project_range`; delimiter trimming reuses the canonical hard-line separator
owner, so CRLF and Unicode mandatory separators do not fall back to a private LF-only policy.

Measurement geometry now closes the adjacent cache/compatibility boundary: the shared projection
owner validates exact shaped source identity plus line/glyph UTF-8 ranges before producing grapheme
advances. `source_grapheme_span` no longer clamps malformed offsets into a one-grapheme estimate;
geometry/fragment paths propagate `BidiInvariant` or `LayoutFailed`, while the legacy scalar
`measured_width` entry returns zero for an invalid run. This is static correctness work only; managed
Cargo, real WGPU/PNG, profile, RSS/power, and Unreal-matched validation remain pending.

VerticalRl rich column projection had the same numeric fail-open pattern: failed `u32/usize`
conversion became `usize::MAX`/`u32::MAX`, and a failed leading-space slice reused the original
start. The column owner now uses checked source-range conversion and checked range publication for
forced, chunk, fallback, and final ranges; malformed input returns `LayoutFailed` before advance
measurement. Normal wrap behavior and legal empty columns are preserved. Static validation only;
managed Cargo/WGPU/profile/power remain pending.

`UiParsedText::project_range` is now the single checked projection owner and returns typed
`TextLayoutError`; table adapters only propagate that result instead of maintaining a second
range-clamping implementation.

Rich advance-index append stages preserve `TextShapingOutcome::Deferred` for font-generation changes;
retryable generation transitions remain visible to the publication owner instead of being collapsed
into `Failed` during metric or glyph-cluster construction.

Horizontal and VerticalRl forced-line/range conversion now shares the checked source-range owner;
invalid UTF-8, overflow, and reverse ranges fail closed before line breaking or materialization.

Forced-line extraction now preallocates from `hard_line_count` and fills the output through the
canonical `visit_hard_lines` pass, removing the intermediate hard-line vector from the rich range
owner; the separate capacity scan is explicit and bounded.

`for_each_validated_rich_run` now lets validation and `RichAdvanceIndex::source_spans` share one
checked run iteration, while `checked_source_range_to_u32` centralizes compact range publication.

Text infrastructure compile cleanup also restored the crate-local glyph projection owner,
unit-typed optional fallback hash branches, explicit font-family identity-map typing, database-only
SDF face-cache calls, scoped runtime-default-face visibility, and cloned Cosmic font snapshots.
The dedicated static compile-contract regression passes 15/15; workspace Cargo remains blocked by
unrelated graphics/core/plugin/dynamic-api diagnostics.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text parser admission and checked identity

Runtime Text now follows the review's typed-error and owner-budget rules at the rich parser boundary.
`RichParseBudget` rejects source before cache allocation, checks visible expansion before append,
bounds recognized token count/per-token bytes and per-token attribute count/bytes before tokenizer
string allocation, and bounds the shared HTML/BBCode ActiveTag stack at a request-configurable depth (default 128);
public parse/compile APIs return `RichTextParseError`. `CompiledRichText` uses one checked conversion
owner for all byte/count/projection indices, and UI publishes stable diagnostic
`ZR-TEXT-LAYOUT-012` instead of saturating or silently dropping malformed identity. Parser admission
state is folder-backed in an 86-line builder child, keeping the semantic parser root at 763 lines.
The old 10,000-depth default-success corpus now expects `ActiveTagDepthBudgetExceeded`; performance
benchmarks must opt into a larger request budget. Static Runtime Text contracts pass 34/34. The latest
E-drive Cargo check failed at an unrelated interface session missing export before Runtime Text;
the workspace gate and all product/performance evidence remain open.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text parser generation wrap alias

- **Finding:** `RichTextParser` assigned process identities with `fetch_add().max(1)` and advanced
  decorator/emoji generations with `wrapping_add` back to one. Numeric exhaustion could therefore
  reuse an older compiled-cache key. Registration mutated the provider registry before generation
  advancement, so a post-mutation exhaustion check would still publish new behavior under stale
  identity.
- **Reference decision:** Unreal's `URichTextBlock` creates widget-owned decorator instances and gives
  strong references to one `FRichTextLayoutMarshaller`; replacement updates that owner. While Zircon
  still has a shared cache, its numeric qualification must be non-reusing until a runtime-context
  service and provider leases replace the process-global boundary.
- **Correction/evidence:** parser identity is now `Option<NonZeroU64>` allocated with atomic
  `fetch_update + checked_add`; exhausted compile fails typed before cache access. Decorator/emoji next
  generations are checked before mutation and fail without publication after `u64::MAX`. Current
  reproducible Runtime Text static tests pass 35/35; rustfmt and no-wrap source guards pass. Rust
  boundary tests are written but unrun. Status:
  `runtime_text_parser_generation_non_reusing_static / cache_wrap_alias_removed /
  managed_validation_pending`; provider lease/revoke, service ownership, retirement, Cargo, WGPU/PNG,
  RSS and power remain open.

  resolution uses a private geometry type and cannot write a marker range back into the semantic model.
  Nested ordered/unordered and marker-range Rust regressions are written; Rustfmt, scoped diff-check, and
  the complete Runtime Text static suite pass 55/55 in 0.239 s.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-037_typed_list_item_metadata_static_complete /
  RRT-P1-040_qualified_publication_and_managed_validation_pending`. Complete typed block-tree/table/HTML
  list support, qualified accessibility children/actions, managed Cargo, WGPU/PNG, timing/allocation/RSS/
  power, commit, and WeCom remain open. No performance improvement is claimed without the managed profile.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text inline image semantics collapsed to U+FFFC

- **Finding:** image runs retained texture/size/baseline but discarded HTML `alt/title`; BBCode had no
  attribute form. The generation-owned semantic projection consequently exposed the object replacement
  character. Fixing this in accessibility would add a second per-snapshot run walker outside parser/cache
  admission and split ownership from layout/paint.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal retains run info, shared text range, image/widget run, marshaller, and text
  layout under one rich-text owner. Zircon likewise compiles image fallback once; adapters read the retained
  product and do not reconstruct runs. Unreal's resource data-table schema is not copied.
- **Correction/evidence:** image runs now retain alternative text and tooltip under existing metadata quota.
  HTML and BBCode attribute forms feed one compiled semantic string with a dedicated byte budget and typed
  overflow error. No-inline artifacts share the visible Arc; inline artifacts account one semantic string
  in residency. Empty alt is decorative, tooltip applies only when alt is absent, and unqualified objects
  cannot leak U+FFFC. Malformed/empty ranges fail artifact construction. Rustfmt/diff-check and the complete
  Runtime Text static suite pass 56/56 in 0.141 s; focused Rust tests are written but unrun.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-029_inline_image_semantic_fallback_static_complete /
  RRT-P1-040_qualified_inline_children_and_managed_validation_pending`. Resource outcome/region/units,
  icon/widget alternatives, qualified child/action identity, managed Cargo, AccessKit, WGPU/PNG, timing/
  allocation/RSS/power, commit, and WeCom remain open.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Interface field schema bypassed authoritative admission

- **Finding:** only VM registration parsed declared field types. Native registrations could publish a
  malformed path when no default forced a type check; duplicate enum values, enum-default membership,
  hint/type compatibility, and numeric metadata were not admitted together. Recursive declaration
  parsing also had no byte/depth bound. World VM paths then validated and rebuilt the same descriptor
  twice, while identical catalog sync still invalidated generation-bound consumers.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal couples `FProperty` storage kind with admitted struct/enum identity and
  builds reflection descriptors before publication. Zircon retains its neutral wire DTO, but selects
  Runtime `TypeRegistry` as the single schema admission owner and uses one parser with separate native
  and VM policies. Editor hints are representation metadata, not a second type authority.
- **Correction/evidence:** complete field vectors now receive bounded O(n)-average admission before
  mutation, with structured type/field errors. The parser is limited to 256 bytes and 16 nested
  containers; checked depth replaces saturation. VM registration/sync consumes one preflight descriptor,
  identical upsert preserves catalog generation, and derive produces typed `List<T>` paths instead of
  silently erasing `Vec<T>` element identity. The F-drive release harness passes 30/30; 21 independent
  1,000,000-parse samples measure P50 482.6 ns and P95 516.2 ns per strict nested parse (range
  435.3..628.4 ns). Focused static gates and targeted rustfmt/diff-check pass. Status:
  `RRI-P1-040_field_admission_source_complete /
  managed_validation_pending`. Stable field IDs, recursive value budgets, managed Rust tests, RSS/power,
  and matched product evidence remain open under their own plan items.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Interface reflected values bypassed bounded admission

- **Finding:** `ReflectedValue` exposed recursive List/Map plus embedded arbitrary JSON with no depth,
  node, string, container, or finite-value contract. World reflection was not the only boundary:
  world-query inspection, dynamic scene, reflected JSON, dynamic component JSON, and VM state/schema
  called adapters or serde directly. The reflected JSON writer had a separate recursive finite walker.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal keeps archive size/error policy at the caller and uses explicit JSON
  scope/element stacks. Zircon likewise separates neutral value representation from Runtime policy.
  Candidate profiling rejected the slower iterator-frame design; a flat work stack matched recursive
  traversal latency while removing dependence on caller stack depth.
- **Correction/evidence:** one RuntimeInterface validator now traverses tagged and JSON graphs with
  checked depth/node/string/container accounting and component-level finite errors. Runtime's single
  policy is applied at schema, editor/remote, inspection, scene, persistence, dynamic JSON, and VM
  boundaries before mutation/publication. The old recursive finite owner is removed. A production-file
  F-drive harness passes 3/3; 21 release-process samples on a 1,153-node mixed graph measure P50
  `37.3 us`, P95 `54.2 us`, P50 `32.3 ns/node`, and a `25.7..113.9 us` untrimmed range. Scoped
  formatting and diff checks pass.
- **Status:** `RRI-P1-042_reflect_value_budget_source_complete /
  managed_and_product_validation_pending`. Product latency/allocation/RSS/power, outer VM/DTO envelope
  limits, and a real paged/bulk transport remain open; the microbenchmark does not close them.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text link admission was discarded before dispatch

- **Finding:** HTML/BBCode admitted an engine-local `ResourceLocator`, immediately converted it to
  `String`, then cloned it through hit/effect/host request. Effect application maintained a second
  hand-written scheme/path validator and fallback parse. This split one security predicate across parser
  and input owners; the existing allocation-free benchmark optimized the duplicate implementation.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal `FSlateHyperlinkRun` retains run metadata and passes that metadata to its
  navigation delegate. Zircon retains that ownership principle but uses a checked typed target rather
  than copying Unreal's arbitrary metadata policy.
- **Correction/evidence:** RuntimeInterface `UiRichLinkTarget` privately shares a canonical locator and
  admits only `res/lib/package/builtin` during construction and deserialization. LinkRef, hit, effect, and
  host request carry it unchanged; application validates only the real node owner. The old validator and
  invalid-target runtime error are removed. Serde retains the `href` scalar wire. Follow-up now retains
  HTML/BBCode tooltip metadata as shared `Arc<str>` through quota, compiled residency, and hit projection;
  it does not confuse rich strings with the surface overlay-ID/timer state. Rustfmt/diff-check and the
  residency calculation now lives in the 76-line `compiled/memory.rs` leaf and leaves the root at
  730 lines. The complete Runtime Text static suite passes 58/58 in the final 0.236 s rerun; Rust
  tests are written but unrun.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-030_typed_target_and_tooltip_metadata_static_complete /
  RRT-P1-040_qualified_link_child_and_managed_validation_pending`. Action kind, visited/disabled state,
  navigation policy, trust/principal, qualified accessibility action identity, managed Cargo/host,
  WGPU/PNG, timing/allocation/RSS/power, commit, and WeCom remain open. No performance gain is claimed.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text format hard cut left stale Rust consumers

- **Finding:** the public/internal format enums had removed unversioned `Html`, `Markdown`, and `BbCode`,
  but seven current-source internal/UI/renderer/transport owners still referenced the deleted
  `RichTextFormat` or `UiRichTextFormat` variants 67 times. The admission fixture also constructed the deleted
  `LinkRef::href` field. The plan's earlier zero-match statement covered the primary parser/API cut but not the
  complete Rust consumer tree, so Cargo would fail before behavior tests.
- **Correction/evidence:** all seven consumers now use `HtmlSubsetV1`, `MarkdownInlineV1`, and `BbCodeV1`;
  the link fixture uses a parser-approved
  `UiRichLinkTarget` and preserves the original 9-byte/12-byte budget assertions. A source-wide static
  word-boundary check rejects deleted format variants, and stale `.href`/constructor scans are zero. The audit
  also replaced 11 non-existent `[link]` artifact fixtures with the registered `[url]` tag and added a compiled
  canonical-target assertion, so those tests no longer compare unknown markup. One inline-widget product fixture
  moved from the rejected `bbcode` wire value to `bbcode_v1`; Runtime authored-value scans are now zero. The
  complete Runtime/RuntimeInterface enum scan returns zero deleted variants. The scoped Runtime Text
infrastructure suite passes 47/47 in the final 1.744 s rerun; focused rustfmt passes.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-024_versioned_format_identity_current_source_consumer_closure_static_complete /
  managed_validation_pending`. This is a compile-closure repair, not a behavior, performance, WGPU, or PNG
  acceptance claim.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text icon metric and paint used different font owners

- **Finding:** `InlineObjectRef::Icon` retained only glyph + family. Horizontal/vertical layout reserved an
  em-like square without shaping; renderer later submitted a separate text batch with no font asset and no
  glyph artifact. Dependency closure ignored the icon font, so measure, paint, fallback, and generation had
  no shared authority.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal `FSlateIcon` identifies a style-set brush, and the rich image decorator
  resolves it into `FSlateImageRun`, whose brush size/lifetime drives both measure and paint. Zircon's primary
  contract should likewise be a typed icon asset; a font-backed icon is allowed only with explicit font
  asset/face lease and canonical shaping.
- **Correction/evidence:** the anonymous branch is hard-cut to `RichIconAssetId` with explicit size, baseline,
  and alternative/decorative text. Controlled locator admission is shared with inline images; horizontal and
  VerticalRl layout consume that geometry, renderer emits an image batch without icon reshaping, and
  `IconAsset` enters the compiled dependency closure. Semantic text, metadata quota, and residency account the
  alternative. UI texture discovery now prefetches the icon; the shared image system invalidates locator resolution
  on `ResourceManagementGeneration` replacement and refreshes the real/fallback GPU texture binding. The compiled
  text artifact intentionally does not pin that render generation. The complete Runtime Text static suite passes
40/40 in the final 0.222 s rerun; focused Rust behavior tests are written but unrun. Intrinsic metric revision
  invalidation, qualified readiness receipt, and font-icon leases remain open.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-028_typed_image_icon_asset_hard_cut_static_complete /
  intrinsic_metric_revision_readiness_font_icon_and_managed_validation_pending`. No benchmark, Cargo, or visual
  evidence is claimed.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text image-only dependency list was falsely generic

- **Finding:** `CompiledRichText::resource_ids()` collected only image textures, while its generic name
  invited future icon/font/widget/decorator identities into the same raw slice. The sole production consumer
  treated every id as a 2D texture, so that extension would route non-textures into texture loading.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal `FSlateImageRun` retains the brush used for measure and paint, `FSlateIcon`
  resolves a style-set brush, and widget decorators construct a real child run. Zircon therefore publishes
  dependencies by admitted kind and refuses to treat a family string, bare widget id, or parser generation
  as a resource lease.
- **Correction/evidence:** `RichTextDependency::{ImageTexture, IconAsset}` is collected once, sorted,
  deduplicated, retained by the compiled artifact, and explicitly matched by downstream kind. The old
  `resource_ids()` API remains removed and residency accounts the typed slice. Construction remains
  `O(R + D log D)` with `O(D)` temporary memory. Rustfmt/diff-check and the complete Runtime Text static
suite pass 40/40 in the final 0.222 s rerun; Rust behavior tests are written but unrun.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-020_typed_image_and_icon_dependency_foundation_static_complete /
  generation_font_widget_decorator_lease_and_managed_validation_pending`. Managed Cargo, real resource outcome,
  WGPU/PNG, profile/RSS/power, commit, and WeCom remain open. No performance or visual gain is claimed.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text inline resource fallback lost its reason

- **Finding:** UI texture discovery reaches the existing scene-resource prepare pass, but
  `ui_texture_id_for_upload` reduces resolution/load/dimension admission to `Option`, and the caller ignores
  `ensure_texture_for_frame`. Unresolved identity, load failure, invalid dimension, and upload failure all reach
  the same fallback texture with no qualified receipt.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal `FSlateImageRun` shares one brush size across measure/paint while the brush and
  renderer own resource/dynamic-load state. Zircon keeps authored size and semantic fallback in the compiled run;
  mutable readiness/failure remains frame resource state and must not trigger recompilation.
- **Correction/evidence:** the scene-resource pass now publishes a typed frame receipt qualified by
  requested/resolved IDs, exact management/readiness generations, prepared revision and frame epoch. Descriptor
  admission and GPU publication reuse one resource snapshot; the renderer only binds an exact-revision `Ready`
  row and otherwise preserves authored geometry with the shared fallback. The dependency collector now returns
  module-owned `UiTextureDependencies`, so frame preparation cannot receive an arbitrary duplicate raw slice;
  its existing hash-set collection and sorted vector remain the sole dedupe work. Receipt construction and row
  mutation are also owner-private while the scene renderer retains read-only receipt access. Focused static
  contracts pass 7/7 and the complete Runtime Text static suite passes 106/106; Rust behavior and product
  evidence are unrun.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-029_frame_qualified_prepare_receipt_static_implemented /
  single_snapshot_and_exact_revision_binding_static_implemented /
  distinct_dependency_owner_invariant_static_implemented / managed_validation_and_profile_pending`.
  The locator-derived compatibility lane remains worst-case `O(D * R + B)` and must not be optimized before the
  planned 1/16/128/512 profile. Managed Cargo, WGPU/PNG, RSS, power, commit, and WeCom remain open.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text compiled cache counters could permanently freeze

- **Finding:** the compiled rich cache was already session/parser owned, but its events were lifetime
  saturating counters. A UI-side sampler subtracted successive copies. Once a counter reached `u64::MAX`,
  every later interval became indistinguishable from zero activity, while parser/provider generation was
  absent from the report.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal's `FShapedTextCache` is an explicitly created cache instance with its own
  key/map/clear lifecycle and font-cache context. Zircon keeps the same instance-lifecycle principle while
  using a bounded interval report suited to its Surface profiling session; it does not copy Unreal stats
  macros or introduce a process-global telemetry owner.
- **Correction/evidence:** cache-local `take_report()` now copies and resets six event counters under the
  cache mutex, preserves residency gauges, reports checked-overflow saturation, and is stamped by the parser
  owner with parser/decorator/emoji generations. The UI sampler type and field are deleted. Profile emission
  currently uses 16 fixed names after the RRT-P1-014 contention extension and contains no markup or dynamic
  tenant label. The current infrastructure static suite passes 36/36 in the final 0.206 s rerun; rustfmt and
  scoped diff-check pass, and old sampler symbols scan to zero.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-022_parser_provider_qualified_reset_snapshot_static_complete /
  project_surface_correlation_and_managed_profile_pending`. This is a telemetry-correctness foundation;
  managed Cargo, matched profile/RSS/power, WGPU/PNG, commit, and WeCom remain open, so no performance or
  visual improvement is claimed.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text single-flight redesign lacked contention evidence

- **Finding:** `OnceLock::get_or_init` can indefinitely block same-key waiters behind a hung first compiler,
  but the cache report could not distinguish warm hits, active compilation, or completed waits. The related
  plan was also stale: decorator panic and metadata/output-budget isolation already existed; only
  non-cooperative deadline/cancel remained open.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal `FShapedTextCache` uses instance-local synchronous `Find -> Add`. That is an
  ownership reference, not permission to copy its calling-model assumptions. Per-caller duplicate parsing
  would amplify CPU and still hang inside the same provider; an arbitrary timeout without a bounded worker
  would abandon the caller but not the work. Both are rejected before measurement.
- **Correction/evidence:** the existing single-flight algorithm is unchanged. Cache-local instrumentation now
  publishes an in-flight gauge and completed waiter count/total/max nanoseconds; initializer-local `Cell`
  attribution avoids counting the actual parser as its own waiter, and an RAII guard owns gauge cleanup.
  The fixed profile set is 16 names with no markup or dynamic label. Static contracts pass 36/36 in 0.206 s;
  rustfmt/diff-check pass; production/tests/profile owners are 541/340/739 lines.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-014_contention_measurement_static_complete /
  bounded_worker_cancellation_and_managed_profile_pending`. Managed contention/fault profile, Cargo,
  allocation/RSS/power, worker/cancel design, commit, and WeCom remain open. No performance gain is claimed.

## 2026-08-30 Reflected field names conflated persistence identity and execution slots

- **Finding:** reflection schema, public read/write, dynamic scene, editor state, and VM migration used
  current field-name strings as identity, while adapters also exposed vector-position `u32` slots. A
  rename therefore appeared as delete/add, and the single-write path performed an O(fields * compared
  bytes) scan before execution. VM string rename patches were not a shared migration authority.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal tagged properties keep `FName` and optional `PropertyGuid` separate,
  resolve GUID to the current property, then apply explicit redirects while retaining ordered bulk
  traversal. Zircon adopts the same ownership split: persistent ID, current/display/alias metadata, and
  dense local slot; the neutral catalog is the only admitted ID-to-slot projection.
- **Correction/evidence:** RuntimeInterface now owns canonical non-nil `ReflectFieldId`; native/script
  derive emit stable identity keys and reject hidden whitespace; registry atomically rejects ID/name/
  alias collisions and builds an adaptive immutable index. WorldReflection component/resource writes
  resolve the stable ID and call slot adapters. Direct production-index source gate passes all entries
  at 0/1/16/512/513/4096 fields plus unknown IDs. In 15 interleaved source-level release samples,
  After the index moved to its final RuntimeInterface catalog owner, the rerun measured 512-field lookup
  P50 from `3034.09` to `23.60 ns/probe` (`128.6x`) and 4096-field lookup from `20721.64` to
  `72.44 ns/probe` (`286.0x`); all raw samples and P95 outliers are retained on F drive.
- **Status:** `RRI-P1-043_field_identity_slot_index_source_foundation_complete /
  public_dto_persistence_vm_managed_product_validation_pending`. The harness uses an equal-width mock
  ID around the production index source and is not full ABI, workload, RSS, power, or Unreal parity
  evidence. Read/write/value DTOs, dynamic scene/editor/VM state, resource reads, string rename removal,
  type/variant IDs, managed Cargo, product profiling, commit, and WeCom remain open.

## 2026-08-30 Reflection registrations had no authoritative catalog snapshot

- **Finding:** RuntimeInterface returned a bare registration vector while Runtime independently owned
  short-name ambiguity and field-slot maps; dynamic-scene and VM migration built separate string rename
  authority. There was no global field-ID gate, dependency closure, or registration-set fingerprint.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal tagged load first resolves Property GUID, then applies owner-scoped property
  redirects, while CoreRedirects bounds chains and reports conflicts. Zircon uses one neutral admitted
  catalog and keeps runtime adapters as a separate execution projection; aliases are importer-only data.
- **Correction/evidence:** `ReflectSchemaCatalog` now admits canonical entries, global IDs, scoped aliases,
  short-name ambiguity and acyclic explicit dependencies; it produces a versioned iterative BLAKE3
  fingerprint and verified snapshot. Runtime deleted its duplicate identity indexes, schema responses bind
  filtered output to the full fingerprint, and dynamic descriptor batches preflight against the current
  catalog plus batch additions before mutation. The direct production admission source gate passes
  canonical/ambiguity/ID-collision/missing-dependency/cycle cases; its first compile found and drove a real
  alias-sort borrow fix. Rustfmt and scoped diff checks pass.
- **Status:** `RRI-P1-045_catalog_and_runtime_projection_source_foundation_complete /
  generated_dependency_persistence_managed_product_validation_pending`. Derive/script dependencies,
  stable-ID public value wire, scene/VM import, managed Cargo, allocation/RSS/power, commit, and WeCom remain
  open.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime Text bidi controls had no authoring evidence owner

- **Finding:** all four rich formats preserved raw bidi controls; HTML numeric entities and BBCode literal
  tags could synthesize the same invisible characters. BBCode treated safe isolation and legacy override as
  equivalent substitutions, while authoring diagnostics were HTML-syntax-only. Shaping correctly executed
  UAX#9 but could not report content provenance or trust.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal Slate separates `TextBiDi` analysis/shaping from logical run ownership and
  later visual block ordering. Zircon keeps that split and assigns source trust/diagnostics to the parser;
  injecting or stripping controls in shaping is rejected because it would fork copy, hit, a11y, and paint
  identity.
- **Correction/evidence:** one parser leaf classifies marks, embedding/pop, overrides, and isolates into stable
  codes 013..016. Plain/Markdown/HTML/BBCode source slices, decoded HTML entities, and BBCode literal tags publish
  exact bounded source ranges without rewriting logical text. `RichTextContentTrust` is now per-compile and
  cache-identifying: default untrusted accepts marks/balanced isolates and rejects legacy controls; explicit
  trusted authoring still requires a balanced stack. A dedicated 125-level budget fails before growth. The
  complete infrastructure static suite passes 38/38 in the final 0.090 s rerun; focused Rust behavior tests are
  written but unrun. Parser root/bidi leaf/root tests/parser-performance tests/bidi tests are
  558/259/195/758/238/215 lines.
- **Status:** `RRT-P1-041_trust_gate_and_balanced_isolation_static_complete /
  managed_copy_a11y_render_and_profile_pending`. Managed Cargo/copy/a11y/WGPU/PNG/profile/RSS/power, commit, and
  WeCom remain open. No security closure, performance gain, or visual result is claimed.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime WGPU standalone UI retained a second submission/completion world

- **Finding:** shared retained UI already submits through the runtime `Arc<WgpuRenderDevice>` and does not
  own completion polling, but `WgpuUiSurfaceRenderer::new_owned` still creates raw instance/adapter/device/
  queue state. Its successful present directly calls `queue.submit` without a ticket, while the optional
  local `GpuReadbackQueue::poll_completed` directly polls that device. Replacing only the submit call would
  leave two completion owners and erase diagnostic/fault/retirement identity.
- **Reference/decision:** Unreal Slate records through the render-thread RHI command list, then central RHI
  ownership finalizes/submits work, transitions/presents the viewport, and defers resource deletion. Zircon
  retains a distinct standalone tool device profile but requires one local `WgpuRenderDevice` to own submit,
  poll, diagnostic, surface and terminal state together; it does not copy Unreal's thread or multi-queue model.
- **Correction/evidence:** the dependency-ordered SUI-0 through SUI-4 design is recorded in
  `docs/plans/optimize/zircon_runtime/90/2026-08-30-standalone-ui-local-submission-completion-hard-cut-design.md`.
  SUI-0 through SUI-3 landed in dependency order: offscreen and standalone share one initial-profile factory; every
  native context has a typed `Arc<WgpuRenderDevice>` owner; raw present submit is removed; local completion
  is polled once before acquire; and the legacy staging queue can only collect after that owner poll. UI image
  pins travel with the native packet and retire by exact ticket through the sole submission callback or fault
  terminalization. The owner/profile contracts pass 11/11 and the SUI-3 contract moved from 0/8 to 8/8;
  exact rustfmt, scoped diff, source guards, structure budgets, and locked metadata pass.
- **Status:** `runtime90_standalone_ui_sui_0_through_sui_3_source_implemented_static_checks_passed_dynamic_validation_pending`.
  Managed Cargo, real WGPU window and fault injection, PNG/RDC, profile, power, commit, and
  WeCom remain open; no performance or visual result is claimed.

## 2026-08-30 Runtime product preparation retained raw queue authority without behavior

- **Finding:** `ResourceStreamer::ensure_scene_resources` forwarded a raw `wgpu::Queue` through the
  frame material path to an unused `_queue` parameter. Separately, product `GpuPassTimer` construction
  accepted the raw queue only to read timestamp period, even though the unique `WgpuRenderDevice` had
  already captured that immutable fact. These signatures enlarged native authority and kept outer queue
  ownership artificially live without representing upload or submission work.
- **Correction/evidence:** PFO-4d4b removes queue from the product scene resource/material chain and
  routes timestamp period from the device owner into the product timer. Test-only material fixtures and
  the legacy standalone UI timer remain explicit exceptions; shared UI query migration is deferred until
  device-level consumer routing exists. The failing-first contract moved from 0/5 to 7/7; exact rustfmt,
  scoped diff, structure budgets, and locked metadata pass.
- **Status:** `runtime90_pfo_4d4b_source_implemented_static_checks_passed_dynamic_validation_pending`.
  Other raw Device/Queue consumers, managed Cargo, WGPU, PNG/RDC, profile, power, commit, and WeCom remain
  open; no submission-count or performance improvement is claimed.
## 2026-08-31 Runtime render-pass native capability scope follow-up

Render-pass native capability的owner收窄与资源创建测量门禁已完成source-only实现：主context
保持scene-renderer ownership，native context不再公开raw `Device`，Hybrid GI与Contact Shadow
通过可计数factory录制资源创建，generic compute的cache miss与dispatch bind group也进入同一
计数；分类结果进入per-pass/frame profile和固定诊断路径。其它内建raw-device helper仍是明确
覆盖缺口；当前迁移上界为89处引用/26个文件，不能解释为每帧create次数。编码顺序、single submission、shader、dispatch与缓存策略未改变；受管Cargo、真实WGPU、PNG/RDC、
RenderDoc、300帧profile、VRAM与功耗仍pending。规范状态与证据见
`docs/plans/optimize/zircon_runtime/90/2026-08-27-pfo-4d-native-resource-write-data-plane-plan.md`
的PFO-4d4z、PFO-4d5a、PFO-4d5b与PFO-4d5c。
